82 |   void add(Node n, Context context) {
83 |     if (!cc.continueProcessing()) {
84 |       return;
85 |     }

88 |     String opstr = NodeUtil.opToStr(type);
89 |     int childCount = n.getChildCount();
90 |     Node first = n.getFirstChild();

93 |     // Handle all binary operators
94 |     if (opstr != null && first != last) {
95 |       Preconditions.checkState(
96 |           childCount == 2,
97 |           "Bad binary operator \"%s\": expected 2 arguments but got %s",
98 |           opstr, childCount);
99 |       int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
100 |       addLeftExpr(first, p, context);
101 |       cc.addOp(opstr, true);
102 | 
103 |       // For right-hand-side of operations, only pass context if it's
104 |       // the IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE one.
105 |       Context rhsContext = getContextForNoInOperator(context);
106 | 
107 |       // Handle associativity.
108 |       // e.g. if the parse tree is a * (b * c),
109 |       // we can simply generate a * b * c.
110 |       if (last.getType() == type &&
111 |           NodeUtil.isAssociative(type)) {
112 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
113 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(n) && NodeUtil.isAssignmentOp(last)) {
114 |         // Assignments are the only right-associative binary operators
115 |         addExpr(last, p, rhsContext);
116 |       } else {
117 |         addExpr(last, p + 1, rhsContext);
118 |       }
119 |       return;
120 |     }

125 |       case Token.TRY: {
126 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getNext().getType() == Token.BLOCK &&
127 |                 !first.getNext().hasMoreThanOneChild());
128 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount >= 2 && childCount <= 3);
129 | 
130 |         add("try");
131 |         add(first, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
132 | 
133 |         // second child contains the catch block, or nothing if there
134 |         // isn't a catch block
135 |         Node catchblock = first.getNext().getFirstChild();
136 |         if (catchblock != null) {
137 |           add(catchblock);
138 |         }
139 | 
140 |         if (childCount == 3) {
141 |           add("finally");
142 |           add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
143 |         }
144 |         break;
145 |       }
146 | 
147 |       case Token.CATCH:
148 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
149 |         add("catch(");
150 |         add(first);
151 |         add(")");
152 |         add(last, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
153 |         break;
154 | 
155 |       case Token.THROW:
156 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
157 |         add("throw");
158 |         add(first);
159 | 
160 |         // Must have a ';' after a throw statement, otherwise safari can't
161 |         // parse this.
162 |         cc.endStatement(true);
163 |         break;
164 | 
165 |       case Token.RETURN:
166 |         add("return");
167 |         if (childCount == 1) {
168 |           add(first);
169 |         } else {
170 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
171 |         }
172 |         cc.endStatement();
173 |         break;
174 | 
175 |       case Token.VAR:
176 |         if (first != null) {
177 |           add("var ");
178 |           addList(first, false, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
179 |         }
180 |         break;
181 | 
182 |       case Token.LABEL_NAME:
183 |         Preconditions.checkState(!n.getString().isEmpty());
184 |         addIdentifier(n.getString());
185 |         break;
186 | 
187 |       case Token.NAME:
188 |         if (first == null || first.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
189 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
190 |         } else {
191 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
192 |           addIdentifier(n.getString());
193 |           cc.addOp("=", true);
194 |           if (first.getType() == Token.COMMA) {
195 |             addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.ASSIGN));
196 |           } else {
197 |             // Add expression, consider nearby code at lowest level of
198 |             // precedence.
199 |             addExpr(first, 0, getContextForNoInOperator(context));
200 |           }
201 |         }
202 |         break;
203 | 
204 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
205 |         add("[");
206 |         addArrayList(first);
207 |         add("]");
208 |         break;
209 | 
210 |       case Token.LP:
211 |         add("(");
212 |         addList(first);
213 |         add(")");
214 |         break;

217 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
218 |         addList(first, false, context);
219 |         break;

224 |         break;

229 |       case Token.BITNOT:
230 |       case Token.POS: {
231 |         // All of these unary operators are right-associative
232 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
233 |         cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
234 |         addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
235 |         break;

238 |       case Token.NEG: {
239 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
240 | 
241 |         // It's important to our sanity checker that the code
242 |         // we print produces the same AST as the code we parse back.
243 |         // NEG is a weird case because Rhino parses "- -2" as "2".
244 |         if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NUMBER) {
245 |           cc.addNumber(-n.getFirstChild().getDouble());
246 |         } else {
247 |           cc.addOp(NodeUtil.opToStrNoFail(type), false);
248 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type));
249 |         }
250 |

252 |       }

254 |       case Token.HOOK: {
255 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
256 |         int p = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
257 |         addLeftExpr(first, p + 1, context);
258 |         cc.addOp("?", true);
259 |         addExpr(first.getNext(), 1);
260 |         cc.addOp(":", true);
261 |         addExpr(last, 1);
262 |         break;

266 |         if (first.getType() != Token.STRING ||

268 |           throw new Error("Expected children to be strings");
269 |         }

272 |

274 |         if (childCount == 2) {
275 |           add(regexp + last.getString());
276 |         } else {
277 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
278 |           add(regexp);
279 |         }
280 |         break;

284 |         break;

287 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
288 |         add(first);

296 |         }

298 |         boolean funcNeedsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
299 |         if (funcNeedsParens) {
300 |           add("(");
301 |         }

312 |         }

319 |         Preconditions.checkState(first.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);

323 |         if (type == Token.GET) {
324 |           // Get methods have no parameters.
325 |           Preconditions.checkState(!first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasChildren());
326 |           add("get ");
327 |         } else {
328 |           // Set methods have one parameter.
329 |           Preconditions.checkState(first.getChildAtIndex(1).hasOneChild());
330 |           add("set ");
331 |         }
332 | 
333 |         // The name is on the GET or SET node.
334 |         String name = n.getString();
335 |         Node fn = first;
336 |         Node parameters = fn.getChildAtIndex(1);
337 |         Node body = fn.getLastChild();
338 | 
339 |         // Add the property name.
340 |         if (!n.isQuotedString() &&
341 |             TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(name) &&
342 |             // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that were
343 |             // unicode escaped.
344 |             NodeUtil.isLatin(name)) {
345 |           add(name);
346 |         } else {
347 |           // Determine if the string is a simple number.
348 |           double d = getSimpleNumber(name);
349 |           if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
350 |             cc.addNumber(d);
351 |           } else {
352 |             add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
353 |           }
354 |         }

356 |         add(parameters);
357 |         add(body, Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK);
358 |         break;

360 |       case Token.SCRIPT:

362 |         if (n.getClass() != Node.class) {
363 |           throw new Error("Unexpected Node subclass.");
364 |         }
365 |         boolean preserveBlock = context == Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
366 |         if (preserveBlock) {
367 |           cc.beginBlock();
368 |         }
369 | 
370 |         boolean preferLineBreaks =
371 |             type == Token.SCRIPT ||

375 |                 n.getParent().getType() == Token.SCRIPT);

377 |           add(c, Context.STATEMENT);

379 |           // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions
380 |           if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {

382 |           }

384 |           if (c.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
385 |             cc.maybeLineBreak();
386 |           }

388 |           // Prefer to break lines in between top-level statements
389 |           // because top level statements are more homogeneous.
390 |           if (preferLineBreaks) {

392 |           }
393 |         }

395 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
396 |         }
397 |         break;
398 |       }

400 |       case Token.FOR:
401 |         if (childCount == 4) {

404 |             add(first, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
405 |           } else {
406 |             addExpr(first, 0, Context.IN_FOR_INIT_CLAUSE);
407 |           }

410 |           add(";");

412 |           add(")");

414 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
415 |         } else {
416 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 3);
417 |           add("for(");
418 |           add(first);
419 |           add("in");

421 |           add(")");
422 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
423 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
424 |         }
425 |         break;
426 | 
427 |       case Token.DO:
428 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
429 |         add("do");
430 |         addNonEmptyStatement(first, Context.OTHER, false);
431 |         add("while(");
432 |         add(last);
433 |         add(")");
434 |         cc.endStatement();
435 |         break;

437 |       case Token.WHILE:
438 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
439 |         add("while(");

441 |         add(")");
442 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
443 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
444 |         break;

447 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
448 |         break;

450 |       case Token.GETPROP: {

458 |         if (needsParens) {

467 |         break;
468 |       }

475 |         add("[");

489 |       case Token.INC:
490 |       case Token.DEC: {
491 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
492 |         String o = type == Token.INC ? "++" : "--";
493 |         int postProp = n.getIntProp(Node.INCRDECR_PROP);
494 |         // A non-zero post-prop value indicates a post inc/dec, default of zero
495 |         // is a pre-inc/dec.
496 |         if (postProp != 0) {
497 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
498 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
499 |         } else {
500 |           cc.addOp(o, false);
501 |           add(first);
502 |         }
503 |         break;
504 |       }
505 | 
506 |       case Token.CALL:
507 |         // We have two special cases here:
508 |         // 1) If the left hand side of the call is a direct reference to eval,
509 |         // then it must have a DIRECT_EVAL annotation. If it does not, then
510 |         // that means it was originally an indirect call to eval, and that
511 |         // indirectness must be preserved.
512 |         // 2) If the left hand side of the call is a property reference,
513 |         // then the call must not a FREE_CALL annotation. If it does, then
514 |         // that means it was originally an call without an explicit this and
515 |         // that must be preserved.
516 |         if (isIndirectEval(first)
517 |             || n.getBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL) && NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
518 |           add("(0,");
519 |           addExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(Token.COMMA));
520 |           add(")");
521 |         } else {
522 |           addLeftExpr(first, NodeUtil.precedence(type), context);
523 |         }
524 |         add("(");
525 |         addList(first.getNext());
526 |         add(")");
527 |         break;
528 | 
529 |       case Token.IF:
530 |         boolean hasElse = childCount == 3;
531 |         boolean ambiguousElseClause =
532 |             context == Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE && !hasElse;
533 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
534 |           cc.beginBlock();
535 |         }
536 | 
537 |         add("if(");
538 |         add(first);
539 |         add(")");
540 | 
541 |         if (hasElse) {
542 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
543 |               first.getNext(), Context.BEFORE_DANGLING_ELSE, false);
544 |           add("else");
545 |           addNonEmptyStatement(
546 |               last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), false);
547 |         } else {
548 |           addNonEmptyStatement(first.getNext(), Context.OTHER, false);
549 |           Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
550 |         }
551 | 
552 |         if (ambiguousElseClause) {
553 |           cc.endBlock();
554 |         }
555 |         break;
556 | 
557 |       case Token.NULL:
558 |       case Token.THIS:
559 |       case Token.FALSE:
560 |       case Token.TRUE:
561 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
562 |         add(Node.tokenToName(type));
563 |         break;
564 | 
565 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
566 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
567 |         add("continue");
568 |         if (childCount == 1) {
569 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
570 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
571 |           }
572 |           add(" ");
573 |           add(first);
574 |         }
575 |         cc.endStatement();
576 |         break;
577 | 
578 |       case Token.DEBUGGER:
579 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 0);
580 |         add("debugger");
581 |         cc.endStatement();
582 |         break;
583 | 
584 |       case Token.BREAK:
585 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount <= 1);
586 |         add("break");
587 |         if (childCount == 1) {
588 |           if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
589 |             throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
590 |           }
591 |           add(" ");
592 |           add(first);
593 |         }
594 |         cc.endStatement();
595 |         break;
596 | 
597 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
598 |         throw new Error("Unexpected EXPR_VOID. Should be EXPR_RESULT.");
599 | 
600 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
601 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
602 |         add(first, Context.START_OF_EXPR);
603 |         cc.endStatement();
604 |         break;
605 | 
606 |       case Token.NEW:
607 |         add("new ");
608 |         int precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(type);
609 | 
610 |         // If the first child contains a CALL, then claim higher precedence
611 |         // to force parentheses. Otherwise, when parsed, NEW will bind to the
612 |         // first viable parentheses (don't traverse into functions).
613 |         if (NodeUtil.containsType(first, Token.CALL, new MatchNotFunction())) {
614 |           precedence = NodeUtil.precedence(first.getType()) + 1;
615 |         }
616 |         addExpr(first, precedence);
617 | 
618 |         // '()' is optional when no arguments are present
619 |         Node next = first.getNext();
620 |         if (next != null) {
621 |           add("(");
622 |           addList(next);
623 |           add(")");
624 |         }
625 |         break;
626 | 
627 |       case Token.STRING:
628 |         if (childCount !=
629 |             ((n.getParent() != null &&
630 |               n.getParent().getType() == Token.OBJECTLIT) ? 1 : 0)) {
631 |           throw new IllegalStateException(
632 |               "Unexpected String children: " + n.getParent().toStringTree());
633 |         }
634 |         add(jsString(n.getString(), outputCharsetEncoder));
635 |         break;
636 | 
637 |       case Token.DELPROP:
638 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
639 |         add("delete ");
640 |         add(first);
641 |         break;
642 | 
643 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT: {
644 |         boolean needsParens = (context == Context.START_OF_EXPR);
645 |         if (needsParens) {
646 |           add("(");
647 |         }
648 |         add("{");
649 |         for (Node c = first; c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
650 |           if (c != first) {
651 |             cc.listSeparator();
652 |           }
653 | 
654 |           if (c.getType() == Token.GET || c.getType() == Token.SET) {
655 |             add(c);
656 |           } else {
657 |             Preconditions.checkState(c.getType() == Token.STRING);
658 |             String key = c.getString();
659 |             // Object literal property names don't have to be quoted if they
660 |             // are not JavaScript keywords
661 |             if (!c.isQuotedString() &&
662 |                 !TokenStream.isKeyword(key) &&
663 |                 TokenStream.isJSIdentifier(key) &&
664 |                 // do not encode literally any non-literal characters that
665 |                 // were unicode escaped.
666 |                 NodeUtil.isLatin(key)) {
667 |               add(key);
668 |             } else {
669 |               // Determine if the string is a simple number.
670 |               double d = getSimpleNumber(key);
671 |               if (!Double.isNaN(d)) {
672 |                 cc.addNumber(d);
673 |               } else {
674 |                 addExpr(c, 1);
675 |               }
676 |             }
677 |             add(":");
678 |             addExpr(c.getFirstChild(), 1);
679 |           }
680 |         }
681 |         add("}");
682 |         if (needsParens) {
683 |           add(")");
684 |         }
685 |         break;
686 |       }
687 | 
688 |       case Token.SWITCH:
689 |         add("switch(");
690 |         add(first);
691 |         add(")");
692 |         cc.beginBlock();
693 |         addAllSiblings(first.getNext());
694 |         cc.endBlock(context == Context.STATEMENT);
695 |         break;
696 | 
697 |       case Token.CASE:
698 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
699 |         add("case ");
700 |         add(first);
701 |         addCaseBody(last);
702 |         break;
703 | 
704 |       case Token.DEFAULT:
705 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 1);
706 |         add("default");
707 |         addCaseBody(first);
708 |         break;
709 | 
710 |       case Token.LABEL:
711 |         Preconditions.checkState(childCount == 2);
712 |         if (first.getType() != Token.LABEL_NAME) {
713 |           throw new Error("Unexpected token type. Should be LABEL_NAME.");
714 |         }
715 |         add(first);
716 |         add(":");
717 |         addNonEmptyStatement(
718 |             last, getContextForNonEmptyExpression(context), true);
719 |         break;
720 | 
721 |       // This node is auto generated in anonymous functions and should just get
722 |       // ignored for our purposes.
723 |       case Token.SETNAME:
724 |         break;
725 | 
726 |       default:
727 |         throw new Error("Unknown type " + type + "\n" + n.toStringTree());
728 |     }
729 | 
730 |     cc.endSourceMapping(n);
731 |   }

===== M0002 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processGeneric(com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 362-369
Documentation:

Method code:
362 |     private Node processGeneric(
363 |         com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Node n) {
364 |       Node node = newNode(transformTokenType(n.getType()));
365 |       for (com.google.javascript.jscomp.mozilla.rhino.Node child : n) {
366 |         node.addChildToBack(transform((AstNode)child));
367 |       }
368 |       return node;
369 |     }

===== M0003 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanVars(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java

Documentation:
/**
   * Scans and gather variables declarations under a Node
   */
Method code:
117 |   private void scanVars(Node n, Node parent) {
118 |     switch (n.getType()) {
119 |       case Token.VAR:
120 |         // Declare all variables. e.g. var x = 1, y, z;
121 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
122 |              child != null;) {
123 |           Node next = child.getNext();
124 |           declareVar(child);
125 |           child = next;
126 |         }
127 |         return;
128 | 
129 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
130 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
131 |           return;
132 |         }
133 | 
134 |         String fnName = n.getFirstChild().getString();
135 |         if (fnName.isEmpty()) {
136 |           // This is invalid, but allow it so the checks can catch it.
137 |           return;
138 |         }
139 |         declareVar(n.getFirstChild());
140 |         return;   // should not examine function's children
141 | 
142 |       case Token.CATCH:
143 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 2);
144 |         Preconditions.checkState(n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME);
145 |         // the first child is the catch var and the third child
146 |         // is the code block
147 | 
148 |         final Node var = n.getFirstChild();
149 |         final Node block = var.getNext();
150 | 
151 |         declareVar(var);
152 |         scanVars(block, n);
153 |         return;  // only one child to scan
154 | 
155 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
156 |         sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
157 |         break;
158 |     }
159 | 
160 |     // Variables can only occur in statement-level nodes, so
161 |     // we only need to traverse children in a couple special cases.
162 |     if (NodeUtil.isControlStructure(n) || NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(n)) {
163 |       for (Node child = n.getFirstChild();
164 |            child != null;) {
165 |         Node next = child.getNext();
166 |         scanVars(child, n);
167 |         child = next;
168 |       }
169 |     }
170 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidateFunctions.findFunctionExpressions(NodeTraversal,Node)

Documentation:
/**
     * Find function expressions that are called directly in the form of
     *   (function(a,b,...){...})(a,b,...)
     * or
     *   (function(a,b,...){...}).call(this,a,b, ...)
     */
Method code:
204 |     public void findFunctionExpressions(NodeTraversal t, Node n) {
205 |       switch (n.getType()) {
206 |         // Functions expressions in the form of:
207 |         //   (function(){})();
208 |         case Token.CALL:
209 |           Node fnNode = null;
210 |           if (n.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
211 |             fnNode = n.getFirstChild();
212 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(n)) {
213 |             Node fnIdentifingNode = n.getFirstChild().getFirstChild();
214 |             if (fnIdentifingNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
215 |               fnNode = fnIdentifingNode;
216 |             }
217 |           }
218 | 
219 |           // If a interesting function was discovered, add it.
220 |           if (fnNode != null) {
221 |             Function fn = new FunctionExpression(fnNode, callsSeen++);
222 |             maybeAddFunction(fn, t.getModule());
223 |             anonFns.put(fnNode, fn.getName());
224 |           }
225 |           break;
226 |       }
227 |     }

===== M0006 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.toString(StringBuilder,boolean,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 925-1043
Documentation:

Method code:
925 |   private void toString(
926 |       StringBuilder sb,
927 |       boolean printSource,
928 |       boolean printAnnotations,
929 |       boolean printType) {
930 |     if (Token.printTrees) {
931 |       sb.append(Token.name(type));
932 |       if (this instanceof StringNode) {
933 |         sb.append(' ');
934 |         sb.append(getString());
935 |       } else if (type == Token.FUNCTION) {
936 |         sb.append(' ');
937 |         // In the case of JsDoc trees, the first child is often not a string
938 |         // which causes exceptions to be thrown when calling toString or
939 |         // toStringTree.
940 |         if (first == null || first.getType() != Token.NAME) {
941 |           sb.append("<invalid>");
942 |         } else {
943 |           sb.append(first.getString());
944 |         }
945 |       } else if (this instanceof ScriptOrFnNode) {
946 |         ScriptOrFnNode sof = (ScriptOrFnNode) this;
947 |         if (this instanceof FunctionNode) {
948 |           FunctionNode fn = (FunctionNode) this;
949 |           sb.append(' ');
950 |           sb.append(fn.getFunctionName());
951 |         }
952 |         if (printSource) {
953 |           sb.append(" [source name: ");
954 |           sb.append(sof.getSourceName());
955 |           sb.append("] [encoded source length: ");
956 |           sb.append(sof.getEncodedSourceEnd() - sof.getEncodedSourceStart());
957 |           sb.append("] [base line: ");
958 |           sb.append(sof.getBaseLineno());
959 |           sb.append("] [end line: ");
960 |           sb.append(sof.getEndLineno());
961 |           sb.append(']');
962 |         }
963 |       } else if (type == Token.NUMBER) {
964 |         sb.append(' ');
965 |         sb.append(getDouble());
966 |       }
967 |       if (printSource) {
968 |         int lineno = getLineno();
969 |         if (lineno != -1) {
970 |           sb.append(' ');
971 |           sb.append(lineno);
972 |         }
973 |       }
974 | 
975 |       if (printAnnotations) {
976 |         int[] keys = getSortedPropTypes();
977 |         for (int i = 0; i < keys.length; i++) {
978 |           int type = keys[i];
979 |           PropListItem x = lookupProperty(type);
980 |           sb.append(" [");
981 |           sb.append(propToString(type));
982 |           sb.append(": ");
983 |           String value;
984 |           switch (type) {
985 |             case TARGETBLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it recurses
986 |               value = "target block property";
987 |               break;
988 |             case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP: // can't add this as it is dull
989 |               value = "last local block";
990 |               break;
991 |             case ISNUMBER_PROP:
992 |               switch (x.intValue) {
993 |                 case BOTH:
994 |                   value = "both";
995 |                   break;
996 |                 case RIGHT:
997 |                   value = "right";
998 |                   break;
999 |                 case LEFT:
1000 |                   value = "left";
1001 |                   break;
1002 |                 default:
1003 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1004 |               }
1005 |               break;
1006 |             case SPECIALCALL_PROP:
1007 |               switch (x.intValue) {
1008 |                 case SPECIALCALL_EVAL:
1009 |                   value = "eval";
1010 |                   break;
1011 |                 case SPECIALCALL_WITH:
1012 |                   value = "with";
1013 |                   break;
1014 |                 default:
1015 |                   // NON_SPECIALCALL should not be stored
1016 |                   throw Kit.codeBug();
1017 |               }
1018 |               break;
1019 |             default:
1020 |               Object obj = x.objectValue;
1021 |               if (obj != null) {
1022 |                 value = obj.toString();
1023 |               } else {
1024 |                 value = String.valueOf(x.intValue);
1025 |               }
1026 |               break;
1027 |           }
1028 |           sb.append(value);
1029 |           sb.append(']');
1030 |         }
1031 |       }
1032 | 
1033 |       if (printType) {
1034 |         if (jsType != null) {
1035 |           String jsTypeString = jsType.toString();
1036 |           if (jsTypeString != null) {
1037 |             sb.append(" : ");
1038 |             sb.append(jsTypeString);
1039 |           }
1040 |         }
1041 |       }
1042 |     }
1043 |   }

   * Builds a single list of all module inputs. Verifies that it contains no
   * duplicates.
   */
Method code:
415 |   private static List<CompilerInput> getAllInputsFromModules(
416 |       List<JSModule> modules) {
417 |     List<CompilerInput> inputs = Lists.newArrayList();
418 |     Map<String, JSModule> inputMap = Maps.newHashMap();
419 |     for (JSModule module : modules) {

429 |     }

555 |   public Scope getScope() {
556 |     Scope scope = scopes.isEmpty() ? null : scopes.peek();
557 |     if (scopeRoots.isEmpty()) {

===== M0029 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.identifierEscape(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1064-1084
Documentation:

Method code:
1064 |   static String identifierEscape(String s) {
1065 |     // First check if escaping is needed at all -- in most cases it isn't.
1066 |     if (NodeUtil.isLatin(s)) {
1067 |       return s;
1068 |     }
1069 | 
1070 |     // Now going through the string to escape non-latin characters if needed.
1071 |     StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
1072 |     for (int i = 0; i < s.length(); i++) {
1073 |       char c = s.charAt(i);
1074 |       // Identifiers should always go to Latin1/ ASCII characters because
1075 |       // different browser's rules for valid identifier characters are
1076 |       // crazy.
1077 |       if (c > 0x1F && c < 0x7F) {
1078 |         sb.append(c);
1079 |       } else {
1080 |         appendHexJavaScriptRepresentation(sb, c);
1081 |       }
1082 |     }
1083 |     return sb.toString();
1084 |   }

===== M0030 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.moveNamedFunctions(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 577-609
Documentation:
/**
     * Move all the functions that are valid at the execution of the first
     * statement of the function to the beginning of the function definition.
     */
Method code:
577 |     private void moveNamedFunctions(Node functionBody) {
578 |       Preconditions.checkState(
579 |           functionBody.getParent().getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
580 |       Node previous = null;
581 |       Node current = functionBody.getFirstChild();
582 |       // Skip any declarations at the beginning of the function body, they
583 |       // are already in the right place.
584 |       while (current != null && NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
585 |         previous = current;
586 |         current = current.getNext();
587 |       }
588 | 
589 |       // Find any remaining declarations and move them.
590 |       Node insertAfter = previous;
591 |       while (current != null) {
592 |         // Save off the next node as the current node maybe removed.
593 |         Node next = current.getNext();
594 |         if (NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(current)) {
595 |           // Remove the declaration from the body.
596 |           Preconditions.checkNotNull(previous);
597 |           functionBody.removeChildAfter(previous);
598 | 
599 |           // Readd the function at the top of the function body (after any
600 |           // previous declarations).
601 |           insertAfter = addToFront(functionBody, current, insertAfter);
602 |           reportCodeChange("Move function declaration not at top of function");
603 |         } else {
604 |           // Update the previous only if the current node hasn't been moved.
605 |           previous = current;
606 |         }
607 |         current = next;
608 |       }
609 |     }

234 |     private void annotateFunctions(Node n, Node parent) {
235 |       JSDocInfo fnInfo = NodeUtil.getFunctionInfo(n);
236 |

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.ObjectType.isUnknownType()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/ObjectType.java

/**
   * We treat this as the unknown type if any of its implicit prototype
   * properties is unknown.
   */
Method code:
467 |   @Override
468 |   public boolean isUnknownType() {
469 |     // If the object is unknown now, check the supertype again,
470 |     // because it might have been resolved since the last check.
471 |     if (unknown) {
472 |       ObjectType implicitProto = getImplicitPrototype();
473 |       if (implicitProto == null ||
474 |           implicitProto.isNativeObjectType()) {
475 |         unknown = false;
476 |       } else {
477 |         unknown = implicitProto.isUnknownType();
478 |       }
479 |     }
480 |     return unknown;
481 |   }

621 |   private void addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
622 |       String propertyName, JSType type) {
623 |     if (type instanceof ObjectType && ((ObjectType) type).hasReferenceName()) {
624 |       Map<String, ObjectType> typeSet =
625 |           eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
626 |       if (typeSet == null) {
627 |         typeSet = Maps.newHashMap();
628 |         eachRefTypeIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
629 |       }
630 |       ObjectType objType = (ObjectType) type;
631 |       typeSet.put(objType.getReferenceName(), objType);
632 |     } else if (type instanceof NamedType) {
633 |       addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(
634 |           propertyName, ((NamedType) type).getReferencedType());
635 |     } else if (type instanceof UnionType) {
636 |       for (JSType alternate : ((UnionType) type).getAlternates()) {
637 |         addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, alternate);
638 |       }
639 |     }
640 |   }

===== M0051 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Compiler.parseInputs()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 1087-1225
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the externs and main inputs.
   *
   * @return A synthetic root node whose two children are the externs root
   *     and the main root
   */
Method code:
1087 |   Node parseInputs() {
1088 |     boolean devMode = options.devMode != DevMode.OFF;
1089 | 
1090 |     // If old roots exist (we are parsing a second time), detach each of the
1091 |     // individual file parse trees.
1092 |     if (externsRoot != null) {
1093 |       externsRoot.detachChildren();
1094 |     }
1095 |     if (jsRoot != null) {
1096 |       jsRoot.detachChildren();
1097 |     }
1098 | 
1099 |     // Parse main js sources.
1100 |     jsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1101 |     jsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1102 | 
1103 |     externsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
1104 |     externsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1105 | 
1106 |     externAndJsRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK, externsRoot, jsRoot);
1107 |     externAndJsRoot.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
1108 | 
1109 |     if (options.tracer.isOn()) {
1110 |       tracker = new PerformanceTracker(jsRoot,
1111 |           options.tracer == TracerMode.ALL);
1112 |       addChangeHandler(tracker.getCodeChangeHandler());
1113 |     }
1114 | 
1115 |     Tracer tracer = newTracer("parseInputs");
1116 | 
1117 |     try {
1118 |       // Parse externs sources.
1119 |       for (CompilerInput input : externs) {
1120 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1121 |         if (hasErrors()) {
1122 |           return null;
1123 |         }
1124 |         externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1125 |       }
1126 | 
1127 |       // Check if the sources need to be re-ordered.
1128 |       if (options.manageClosureDependencies) {
1129 |         for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1130 |           input.setCompiler(this);
1131 | 
1132 |           // Forward-declare all the provided types, so that they
1133 |           // are not flagged even if they are dropped from the process.
1134 |           for (String provide : input.getProvides()) {
1135 |             getTypeRegistry().forwardDeclareType(provide);
1136 |           }
1137 |         }
1138 | 
1139 |         try {
1140 |           inputs =
1141 |               (moduleGraph == null ? new JSModuleGraph(modules) : moduleGraph)
1142 |               .manageDependencies(
1143 |                   options.manageClosureDependenciesEntryPoints, inputs);
1144 |         } catch (CircularDependencyException e) {
1145 |           report(JSError.make(
1146 |               JSModule.CIRCULAR_DEPENDENCY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1147 |           return null;
1148 |         } catch (MissingProvideException e) {
1149 |           report(JSError.make(
1150 |               MISSING_ENTRY_ERROR, e.getMessage()));
1151 |           return null;
1152 |         }
1153 |       }
1154 | 
1155 |       // Check if inputs need to be rebuilt from modules.
1156 |       boolean staleInputs = false;
1157 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1158 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1159 | 
1160 |         // Inputs can have a null AST during initial parse.
1161 |         if (n == null) {
1162 |           continue;
1163 |         }
1164 | 
1165 |         if (n.getJSDocInfo() != null) {
1166 |           JSDocInfo info = n.getJSDocInfo();
1167 |           if (info.isExterns()) {
1168 |             // If the input file is explicitly marked as an externs file, then
1169 |             // assume the programmer made a mistake and throw it into
1170 |             // the externs pile anyways.
1171 |             externsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1172 |             input.setIsExtern(true);
1173 | 
1174 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1175 | 
1176 |             externs.add(input);
1177 |             staleInputs = true;
1178 |           } else if (info.isNoCompile()) {
1179 |             input.getModule().remove(input);
1180 |             staleInputs = true;
1181 |           }
1182 |         }
1183 |       }
1184 | 
1185 |       if (staleInputs) {
1186 |         fillEmptyModules(modules);
1187 |         rebuildInputsFromModules();
1188 |       }
1189 | 
1190 |       // Build the AST.
1191 |       for (CompilerInput input : inputs) {
1192 |         Node n = input.getAstRoot(this);
1193 |         if (n == null) {
1194 |           continue;
1195 |         }
1196 | 
1197 |         if (devMode) {
1198 |           runSanityCheck();
1199 |           if (hasErrors()) {
1200 |             return null;
1201 |           }
1202 |         }
1203 | 
1204 |         if (options.sourceMapOutputPath != null ||
1205 |             options.nameReferenceReportPath != null) {
1206 | 
1207 |           // Annotate the nodes in the tree with information from the
1208 |           // input file. This information is used to construct the SourceMap.
1209 |           SourceInformationAnnotator sia =
1210 |               new SourceInformationAnnotator(
1211 |                   input.getName(), options.devMode != DevMode.OFF);
1212 |           NodeTraversal.traverse(this, n, sia);
1213 |         }
1214 | 
1215 |         jsRoot.addChildToBack(n);
1216 |       }
1217 | 
1218 |       if (hasErrors()) {
1219 |         return null;
1220 |       }
1221 |       return externAndJsRoot;
1222 |     } finally {
1223 |       stopTracer(tracer, "parseInputs");
1224 |     }
1225 |   }

152 |   public void validateExpression(Node n) {
153 |     switch (n.getType()) {
154 |       // Childless expressions
155 |       case Token.FALSE:
156 |       case Token.NULL:
157 |       case Token.THIS:
158 |       case Token.TRUE:
159 |         validateChildless(n);
160 |         return;
161 | 
162 |       // General uniary ops
163 |       case Token.DELPROP:
164 |       case Token.POS:
165 |       case Token.NEG:
166 |       case Token.NOT:
167 |       case Token.INC:
168 |       case Token.DEC:
169 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
170 |       case Token.VOID:
171 |       case Token.BITNOT:
172 |         validateUnaryOp(n);
173 |         return;
174 | 
175 |       // General binary ops
176 |       case Token.COMMA:
177 |       case Token.OR:
178 |       case Token.AND:
179 |       case Token.BITOR:
180 |       case Token.BITXOR:
181 |       case Token.BITAND:
182 |       case Token.EQ:
183 |       case Token.NE:
184 |       case Token.SHEQ:
185 |       case Token.SHNE:
186 |       case Token.LT:
187 |       case Token.GT:
188 |       case Token.LE:
189 |       case Token.GE:
190 |       case Token.INSTANCEOF:
191 |       case Token.IN:
192 |       case Token.LSH:
193 |       case Token.RSH:
194 |       case Token.URSH:
195 |       case Token.SUB:
196 |       case Token.ADD:
197 |       case Token.MUL:
198 |       case Token.MOD:
199 |       case Token.DIV:
200 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
201 |         return;
202 | 
203 |       // Assignments
204 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
205 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITOR:
206 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
207 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITAND:
208 |       case Token.ASSIGN_LSH:
209 |       case Token.ASSIGN_RSH:
210 |       case Token.ASSIGN_URSH:
211 |       case Token.ASSIGN_ADD:
212 |       case Token.ASSIGN_SUB:
213 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MUL:
214 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:
215 |       case Token.ASSIGN_MOD:
216 |         validateAssignmentExpression(n);
217 |         return;
218 | 
219 |       case Token.HOOK:
220 |         validateTrinaryOp(n);
221 |         return;
222 | 
223 |       // Node types that require special handling
224 |       case Token.STRING:
225 |         validateString(n);
226 |         return;
227 | 
228 |       case Token.NUMBER:
229 |         validateNumber(n);
230 |         return;
231 | 
232 |       case Token.NAME:
233 |         validateName(n);
234 |         return;
235 | 
236 |       case Token.GETELEM:
237 |         validateBinaryOp(n);
238 |         return;
239 | 
240 |       case Token.GETPROP:
241 |         validateGetProp(n);
242 |         return;
243 | 
244 |       case Token.ARRAYLIT:
245 |         validateArrayLit(n);
246 |         return;
247 | 
248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         validateObjectLit(n);
250 |         return;
251 | 
252 |       case Token.REGEXP:
253 |         validateRegExpLit(n);
254 |         return;
255 | 
256 |       case Token.CALL:
257 |         validateCall(n);
258 |         return;
259 | 
260 |       case Token.NEW:
261 |         validateNew(n);
262 |         return;
263 | 
264 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
265 |         validateFunctionExpression(n);
266 |         return;
267 | 
268 |       default:
269 |         violation("Expected expression but was "
270 |             + Node.tokenToName(n.getType()), n);
271 |     }
272 |   }

===== M0065 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseFunction(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 470-505
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a function.
   */
Method code:
470 |   private void traverseFunction(Node n, Node parent) {
471 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getChildCount() == 3);
472 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
473 | 
474 |     final Node fnName = n.getFirstChild();
475 | 
476 |     boolean isFunctionExpression = (parent != null)
477 |         && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n);
478 | 
479 |     if (!isFunctionExpression) {
480 |       // Functions declarations are in the scope containing the declaration.
481 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
482 |     }
483 | 
484 |     curNode = n;
485 |     pushScope(n);
486 | 
487 |     if (isFunctionExpression) {
488 |       // Function expression names are only accessible within the function
489 |       // scope.
490 |       traverseBranch(fnName, n);
491 |     }
492 | 
493 |     final Node args = fnName.getNext();
494 |     final Node body = args.getNext();
495 | 
496 |     // Args
497 |     traverseBranch(args, n);
498 | 
499 |     // Body
500 |     Preconditions.checkState(body.getNext() == null &&
501 |             body.getType() == Token.BLOCK);
502 |     traverseBranch(body, n);
503 | 
504 |     popScope();
505 |   }

===== M0070 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.stop(int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 406-433
Documentation:
/**
   * Stop the trace.
   * This may only be done once and must be done from the same thread
   * that started it.
   * @param silence_threshold Traces for time less than silence_threshold
   * ms will be left out of the trace report. A value of -1 indicates
   * that the current ThreadTrace silence_threshold should be used.
   * @return The time that this trace actually ran
   */
Method code:
406 |   long stop(int silence_threshold) {
407 |     Preconditions.checkState(Thread.currentThread() == startThread);
408 | 
409 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
410 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
411 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
412 |       return 0;
413 |     }
414 | 
415 |     stopTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
416 |     if (extraTracingValues != null) {
417 |       // We use extraTracingValues.length rather than
418 |       // extraTracingStatistics.size() because a new statistic may
419 |       // have been added
420 |       for (int i = 0; i < extraTracingValues.length; i++) {
421 |         long value = extraTracingStatistics.get(i).stop(startThread);
422 |         extraTracingValues[i] = value - extraTracingValues[i];
423 |       }
424 |     }
425 | 
426 |     // Do nothing if the thread trace was not initialized.
427 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
428 |       return 0;
429 |     }
430 | 
431 |     trace.endEvent(this, silence_threshold);
432 |     return stopTimeMs - startTimeMs;
433 |   }

===== M0077 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 105-139
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public boolean shouldTraverse(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
107 | 
108 |     switch (n.getType()) {
109 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
110 |         {
111 |           // Add recursive function name, if needed.
112 |           // NOTE: "enterScope" is called after we need to pick up this name.
113 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
114 | 
115 |           // If needed, add the function recursive name.
116 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
117 |           if (name != null && !name.isEmpty() && parent != null
118 |               && !NodeUtil.isFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
119 |             renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
120 |           }
121 | 
122 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
123 |         }
124 |         break;
125 | 
126 |       case Token.CATCH:
127 |         {
128 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
129 | 
130 |           String name = n.getFirstChild().getString();
131 |           renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
132 | 
133 |           nameStack.push(renamer);
134 |         }
135 |         break;
136 |     }
137 | 
138 |     return true;
139 |   }

757 |   private void validateChildCount(Node n, int i) {
758 |     boolean valid = false;
759 |     if (i == 0) {
760 |       valid = !n.hasChildren();
761 |     } else if (i == 1) {
762 |       valid = n.hasOneChild();
763 |     } else {
764 |       valid = (n.getChildCount() == i);
765 |     }
766 |     if (!valid) {
767 |       violation(

769 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
770 |     }
771 |   }

===== M0102 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionToBlockMutator.replaceReturns(Node,String,String,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionToBlockMutator.java
Original range: 256-305
Documentation:
/**
   *  Convert returns to assignments and breaks, as needed.
   *  For example, with a lableName of 'foo':
   *    {
   *      return a;
   *    }
   *  becomes:
   *    foo: {
   *      a;
   *      break foo;
   *    }
   *  or
   *    foo: {
   *      resultName = a;
   *      break foo;
   *    }
   *
   * @param resultMustBeSet Whether the result must always be set to a value.
   * @return The node containing the transformed block, this may be different
   *     than the passed in node 'block'.
   */
Method code:
256 |   private static Node replaceReturns(
257 |       Node block, String resultName, String labelName,
258 |       boolean resultMustBeSet) {
259 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(block);
260 |     Preconditions.checkNotNull(labelName);
261 | 
262 |     Node root = block;
263 | 
264 |     boolean hasReturnAtExit = false;
265 |     int returnCount = NodeUtil.getNodeTypeReferenceCount(
266 |         block, Token.RETURN, new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
267 |     if (returnCount > 0) {
268 |       hasReturnAtExit = hasReturnAtExit(block);
269 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Simpler not to special case this,
270 |       // and let it be optimized later.
271 |       if (hasReturnAtExit) {
272 |         convertLastReturnToStatement(block, resultName);
273 |         returnCount--;
274 |       }
275 | 
276 |       if (returnCount > 0) {
277 |         // A label and breaks are needed.
278 | 
279 |         // Add the breaks
280 |         replaceReturnWithBreak(block, null, resultName, labelName);
281 | 
282 |         // Add label
283 |         Node label = new Node(Token.LABEL).copyInformationFrom(block);
284 |         Node name = Node.newString(Token.LABEL_NAME, labelName)
285 |             .copyInformationFrom(block);
286 |         label.addChildToFront(name);
287 |         label.addChildToBack(block);
288 | 
289 |         Node newRoot = new Node(Token.BLOCK).copyInformationFrom(block);
290 |         newRoot.addChildrenToBack(label);
291 | 
292 | 
293 |         // The label is now the root.
294 |         root = newRoot;
295 |       }
296 |     }
297 | 
298 |     // If there wasn't an return at the end of the function block, and we need
299 |     // a result, add one to the block.
300 |     if (resultMustBeSet && !hasReturnAtExit && resultName != null) {
301 |       addDummyAssignment(block, resultName);
302 |     }
303 | 
304 |     return root;
305 |   }

===== M0103 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.mimimizeCost(FunctionState)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 678-692
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the function is worth inlining and potentially
   * trims references that increase the cost.
   * @return Whether inlining the references lowers the overall cost.
   */
Method code:
678 |   private boolean mimimizeCost(FunctionState fs) {
679 |     if (!inliningLowersCost(fs)) {
680 |       // Try again without Block inlining references
681 |       if (fs.hasBlockInliningReferences()) {
682 |         fs.setRemove(false);
683 |         fs.removeBlockInliningReferences();
684 |         if (!fs.hasReferences() || !inliningLowersCost(fs)) {
685 |           return false;
686 |         }
687 |       } else {
688 |         return false;
689 |       }
690 |     }
691 |     return true;
692 |   }

===== M0108 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.process(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 105-142
Documentation:

Method code:
105 |   @Override
106 |   public void process(Node externs, Node root) {
107 |     Preconditions.checkState(compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized());
108 | 
109 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root, new FindCandidateFunctions());
110 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
111 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
112 |     }
113 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
114 |        new FindCandidatesReferences(fns, anonFns));
115 |     trimCanidatesNotMeetingMinimumRequirements();
116 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
117 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
118 |     }
119 | 
120 |     // Store the set of function names eligible for inlining and use this to
121 |     // prevent function names from being moved into temporaries during
122 |     // expression decomposition. If this movement were allowed it would prevent
123 |     // the Inline callback from finding the function calls.
124 |     //
125 |     // This pass already assumes these are constants, so this is safe for anyone
126 |     // using function inlining.
127 |     //
128 |     Set<String> fnNames = Sets.newHashSet(fns.keySet());
129 |     injector.setKnownConstants(fnNames);
130 | 
131 |     trimCanidatesUsingOnCost();
132 |     if (fns.isEmpty()) {
133 |       return;  // Nothing left to do.
134 |     }
135 |     resolveInlineConflicts();
136 |     decomposeExpressions(fnNames);
137 |     NodeTraversal.traverse(compiler, root,
138 |         new CallVisitor(
139 |             fns, anonFns, new Inline(injector, specializationState)));
140 | 
141 |     removeInlinedFunctions();
142 |   }

===== M0112 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.isCandidateFunction(Function)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 327-353
Documentation:
/**
   * Checks if the given function matches the criteria for an inlinable
   * function.
   */
Method code:
327 |   private boolean isCandidateFunction(Function fn) {
328 |     // Don't inline exported functions.
329 |     String fnName = fn.getName();
330 |     if (compiler.getCodingConvention().isExported(fnName)) {
331 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Should we allow internal references to be inlined?
332 |       // An exported name can be replaced externally, any inlined instance
333 |       // would not reflect this change.
334 |       // To allow inlining we need to be able to distinguish between exports
335 |       // that are used in a read-only fashion and those that can be replaced
336 |       // by external definitions.
337 |       return false;
338 |     }
339 | 
340 |     // Don't inline this special function
341 |     if (RenameProperties.RENAME_PROPERTY_FUNCTION_NAME.equals(fnName)) {
342 |       return false;
343 |     }
344 | 
345 |     // Don't inline if we are specializing and the function can't be fixed up
346 |     if (specializationState != null &&
347 |         !specializationState.canFixupFunction(fn.getFunctionNode())) {
348 |       return false;
349 |     }
350 | 
351 |     Node fnNode = fn.getFunctionNode();
352 |     return injector.doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(fnName, fnNode);
353 |   }

===== M0114 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 474-497
Documentation:
/**
   * Returns true if this is an immutable value.
   */
Method code:
474 |   static boolean isImmutableValue(Node n) {
475 |     switch (n.getType()) {
476 |       case Token.STRING:
477 |       case Token.NUMBER:
478 |       case Token.NULL:
479 |       case Token.TRUE:
480 |       case Token.FALSE:
481 |         return true;
482 |       case Token.NOT:
483 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
484 |       case Token.VOID:
485 |       case Token.NEG:
486 |         return isImmutableValue(n.getFirstChild());
487 |       case Token.NAME:
488 |         String name = n.getString();
489 |         // We assume here that programs don't change the value of the keyword
490 |         // undefined to something other than the value undefined.
491 |         return "undefined".equals(name)
492 |             || "Infinity".equals(name)
493 |             || "NaN".equals(name);
494 |     }
495 | 
496 |     return false;
497 |   }

===== M0122 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.estimateCallCost(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 784-802
Documentation:
/**
   * Gets an estimate of the cost in characters of making the function call:
   * the sum of the identifiers and the separators.
   * @param referencesThis
   */
Method code:
784 |   private static int estimateCallCost(Node fnNode, boolean referencesThis) {
785 |     Node argsNode = NodeUtil.getFnParameters(fnNode);
786 |     int numArgs = argsNode.getChildCount();
787 | 
788 |     int callCost = NAME_COST_ESTIMATE + PAREN_COST;
789 |     if (numArgs > 0) {
790 |       callCost += (numArgs * NAME_COST_ESTIMATE) + ((numArgs - 1) * COMMA_COST);
791 |     }
792 | 
793 |     if (referencesThis) {
794 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Update this if we start supporting inlining
795 |       // other functions that reference this.
796 |       // The only functions that reference this that are currently inlined
797 |       // are those that are called via ".call" with an explicit "this".
798 |       callCost += 5 + 5;  // ".call" + "this,"
799 |     }
800 | 
801 |     return callCost;
802 |   }

===== M0126 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.UnionTypeBuilder.addAlternate(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/UnionTypeBuilder.java
Original range: 95-156
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds an alternate to the union type under construction. Returns this
   * for easy chaining.
   */
Method code:
95 |   UnionTypeBuilder addAlternate(JSType alternate) {
96 |     // build() returns the bottom type by default, so we can
97 |     // just bail out early here.
98 |     if (alternate.isNoType()) {
99 |       return this;
100 |     }
101 | 
102 |     isAllType = isAllType || alternate.isAllType();
103 | 
104 |     boolean isAlternateUnknown = alternate instanceof UnknownType;
105 |     isNativeUnknownType = isNativeUnknownType || isAlternateUnknown;
106 |     if (isAlternateUnknown) {
107 |       areAllUnknownsChecked = areAllUnknownsChecked &&
108 |           alternate.isCheckedUnknownType();
109 |     }
110 |     if (!isAllType && !isNativeUnknownType) {
111 |       if (alternate instanceof UnionType) {
112 |         UnionType union = (UnionType) alternate;
113 |         for (JSType unionAlt : union.getAlternates()) {
114 |           addAlternate(unionAlt);
115 |         }
116 |       } else {
117 |         if (alternates.size() > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
118 |           return this;
119 |         }
120 | 
121 |         // Look through the alternates we've got so far,
122 |         // and check if any of them are duplicates of
123 |         // one another.
124 |         Iterator<JSType> it = alternates.iterator();
125 |         while (it.hasNext()) {
126 |           JSType current = it.next();
127 | 
128 |           // Unknown and NoResolved types may just be names that haven't
129 |           // been resolved yet. So keep these in the union, and just use
130 |           // equality checking for simple de-duping.
131 |           if (alternate.isUnknownType() ||
132 |               current.isUnknownType() ||
133 |               alternate.isNoResolvedType() ||
134 |               current.isNoResolvedType()) {
135 |             if (alternate.isEquivalentTo(current)) {
136 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
137 |               return this;
138 |             }
139 |           } else {
140 |             if (alternate.isSubtype(current)) {
141 |               // Alternate is unnecessary.
142 |               return this;
143 |             } else if (current.isSubtype(alternate)) {
144 |               // Alternate makes current obsolete
145 |               it.remove();
146 |             }
147 |           }
148 |         }
149 |         alternates.add(alternate);
150 |         result = null; // invalidate the memoized result
151 |       }
152 |     } else {
153 |       result = null;
154 |     }
155 |     return this;
156 |   }

451 |       }

===== M0133 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.maybeAddFunction(Function,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 234-300
Documentation:
/**
   * Updates the FunctionState object for the given function. Checks if the
   * given function matches the criteria for an inlinable function.
   */
Method code:
234 |   private void maybeAddFunction(Function fn, JSModule module) {
235 |     String name = fn.getName();
236 |     FunctionState fs = getOrCreateFunctionState(name);
237 | 
238 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Maybe "smarten" FunctionState by adding this logic
239 |     // to it?
240 | 
241 |     // If the function has multiple definitions, don't inline it.
242 |     if (fs.hasExistingFunctionDefinition()) {
243 |       fs.setInline(false);
244 |     } else {
245 |       // verify the function hasn't already been marked as "don't inline"
246 |       if (fs.canInline()) {
247 |         // store it for use when inlining.
248 |         fs.setFn(fn);
249 |         if (injector.isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(
250 |             fn.getFunctionNode())) {
251 |           fs.inlineDirectly(true);
252 |         }
253 | 
254 |         // verify the function meets all the requirements.
255 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Minimum requirement checks are about 5% of the
256 |         // runtime cost of this pass.
257 |         if (!isCandidateFunction(fn)) {
258 |           // It doesn't meet the requirements.
259 |           fs.setInline(false);
260 |         }
261 | 
262 |         // Set the module and gather names that need temporaries.
263 |         if (fs.canInline()) {
264 |           fs.setModule(module);
265 | 
266 |           Node fnNode = fn.getFunctionNode();
267 |           Set<String> namesToAlias =
268 |               FunctionArgumentInjector.findModifiedParameters(fnNode);
269 |           if (!namesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
270 |             fs.inlineDirectly(false);
271 |             fs.setNamesToAlias(namesToAlias);
272 |           }
273 | 
274 |           Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
275 |           if (NodeUtil.referencesThis(block)) {
276 |             fs.setReferencesThis(true);
277 |           }
278 | 
279 |           if (NodeUtil.containsFunction(block)) {
280 |             fs.setHasInnerFunctions(true);
281 |             // If there are inner functions, we can inline into global scope
282 |             // if there are no local vars or named functions.
283 |             // TODO(johnlenz): this can be improved by looking at the possible
284 |             // values for locals.  If there are simple values, or constants
285 |             // we could still inline.
286 |             if (hasLocalNames(fnNode)) {
287 |               fs.setInline(false);
288 |             }
289 |           }
290 |         }
291 | 
292 |         // Check if block inlining is allowed.
293 |         if (fs.canInline() && !fs.canInlineDirectly()) {
294 |           if (!blockFunctionInliningEnabled) {
295 |             fs.setInline(false);
296 |           }
297 |         }
298 |       }
299 |     }
300 |   }

===== M0145 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.PrototypeObjectType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/PrototypeObjectType.java
Original range: 422-466
Documentation:

Method code:
422 |   @Override
423 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
424 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
425 |       return true;
426 |     }
427 | 
428 |     // Union types
429 |     if (that instanceof UnionType) {
430 |       // The static {@code JSType.isSubtype} check already decomposed
431 |       // union types, so we don't need to check those again.
432 |       return false;
433 |     }
434 | 
435 |     // record types
436 |     if (that instanceof RecordType) {
437 |       return RecordType.isSubtype(this, (RecordType) that);
438 |     }
439 | 
440 |     // Interfaces
441 |     // Find all the interfaces implemented by this class and compare each one
442 |     // to the interface instance.
443 |     ObjectType thatObj = that.toObjectType();
444 |     ObjectType thatCtor = thatObj == null ? null : thatObj.getConstructor();
445 |     if (thatCtor != null && thatCtor.isInterface()) {
446 |       Iterable<ObjectType> thisInterfaces = getCtorImplementedInterfaces();
447 |       for (ObjectType thisInterface : thisInterfaces) {
448 |         if (thisInterface.isSubtype(that)) {
449 |           return true;
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 |     }
453 | 
454 |     // other prototype based objects
455 |     if (that != null) {
456 |       if (isUnknownType() || implicitPrototypeChainIsUnknown()) {
457 |         // If unsure, say 'yes', to avoid spurious warnings.
458 |         // TODO(user): resolve the prototype chain completely in all cases,
459 |         // to avoid guessing.
460 |         return true;
461 |       }
462 |       return this.isImplicitPrototype(thatObj);
463 |     }
464 | 
465 |     return false;
466 |   }

===== M0153 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.enterScope(NodeTraversal)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.java
Original range: 64-96
Documentation:

Method code:
64 |   @Override
65 |   public void enterScope(NodeTraversal t) {
66 |     Node declarationRoot = t.getScopeRoot();
67 |     Renamer renamer;
68 |     if (nameStack.isEmpty()) {
69 |       // If the contextual renamer is being used the starting context can not
70 |       // be a function.
71 |       Preconditions.checkState(
72 |           declarationRoot.getType() != Token.FUNCTION ||
73 |           !(rootRenamer instanceof ContextualRenamer));
74 |       Preconditions.checkState(t.inGlobalScope());
75 |       renamer = rootRenamer;
76 |     } else {
77 |       renamer = nameStack.peek().forChildScope();
78 |     }
79 | 
80 |     if (declarationRoot.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
81 |       // Add the function parameters
82 |       Node fnParams = declarationRoot.getFirstChild().getNext();
83 |       for (Node c = fnParams.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
84 |         String name = c.getString();
85 |         renamer.addDeclaredName(name);
86 |       }
87 | 
88 |       // Add the function body declarations
89 |       Node functionBody = declarationRoot.getLastChild();
90 |       findDeclaredNames(functionBody, null, renamer);
91 |     } else {
92 |       // Add the block declarations
93 |       findDeclaredNames(declarationRoot, null, renamer);
94 |     }
95 |     nameStack.push(renamer);
96 |   }

===== M0164 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeConsumer.addNumber(double)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeConsumer.java
Original range: 233-259
Documentation:

Method code:
233 |   void addNumber(double x) {
234 |     // This is not pretty printing. This is to prevent misparsing of x- -4 as
235 |     // x--4 (which is a syntax error).
236 |     char prev = getLastChar();
237 |     if (x < 0 && prev == '-') {
238 |       add(" ");
239 |     }
240 | 
241 |     if ((long) x == x) {
242 |       long value = (long) x;
243 |       long mantissa = value;
244 |       int exp = 0;
245 |       if (Math.abs(x) >= 100) {
246 |         while (mantissa / 10 * Math.pow(10, exp + 1) == value) {
247 |           mantissa /= 10;
248 |           exp++;
249 |         }
250 |       }
251 |       if (exp > 2) {
252 |         add(Long.toString(mantissa) + "E" + Integer.toString(exp));
253 |       } else {
254 |         add(Long.toString(value));
255 |       }
256 |     } else {
257 |       add(String.valueOf(x));
258 |     }
259 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1409-1428
Documentation:
/**
   * Compare this node to node2 recursively and return the first pair of nodes
   * that differs doing a preorder depth-first traversal. Package private for
   * testing. Returns null if the nodes are equivalent.
   */
Method code:
1409 |   NodeMismatch checkTreeEqualsImpl(Node node2) {
1410 |     if (!isEquivalentTo(node2, false, false)) {
1411 |       return new NodeMismatch(this, node2);
1412 |     }
1413 | 
1414 |     NodeMismatch res = null;
1415 |     Node n, n2;
1416 |     for (n = first, n2 = node2.first;
1417 |          res == null && n != null;
1418 |          n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1419 |       if (node2 == null) {
1420 |         throw new IllegalStateException();
1421 |       }
1422 |       res = n.checkTreeEqualsImpl(n2);
1423 |       if (res != null) {
1424 |         return res;
1425 |       }
1426 |     }
1427 |     return res;
1428 |   }

===== M0175 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(FunctionState)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 733-759
Documentation:
/**
   * @see #resolveInlineConflicts
   */
Method code:
733 |   private void resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(FunctionState fs) {
734 |     // Functions that aren't referenced don't cause conflicts.
735 |     if (!fs.hasReferences()) {
736 |       return;
737 |     }
738 | 
739 |     Node fnNode = fs.getFn().getFunctionNode();
740 |     Set<String> names = findCalledFunctions(fnNode);
741 |     if (!names.isEmpty()) {
742 |       // Prevent the removal of the referenced functions.
743 |       for (String name : names) {
744 |         FunctionState fsCalled = fns.get(name);
745 |         if (fsCalled != null && fsCalled.canRemove()) {
746 |           fsCalled.setRemove(false);
747 |           // For functions that can no longer be removed, check if they should
748 |           // still be inlined.
749 |           if (!mimimizeCost(fsCalled)) {
750 |             // It can't be inlined remove it from the list.
751 |             fsCalled.setInline(false);
752 |           }
753 |         }
754 |       }
755 | 
756 |       // Make a copy of the Node, so it isn't changed by other inlines.
757 |       fs.setSafeFnNode(fs.getFn().getFunctionNode().cloneTree());
758 |     }
759 |   }

260 |   public void traverseRoots(List<Node> roots) {
261 |     if (roots.isEmpty()) {
262 |       return;
263 |     }

===== M0183 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(Node,Node,Supplier<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionArgumentInjector.java
Original range: 81-121
Documentation:
/**
   * Get a mapping for function parameter names to call arguments.
   */
Method code:
81 |   static LinkedHashMap<String, Node> getFunctionCallParameterMap(
82 |       Node fnNode, Node callNode, Supplier<String> safeNameIdSupplier) {
83 |     // Create an argName -> expression map
84 |     // NOTE: A linked map is created here to provide ordering.
85 |     LinkedHashMap<String, Node> argMap = Maps.newLinkedHashMap();
86 | 
87 |     // CALL NODE: [ NAME, ARG1, ARG2, ... ]
88 |     Node cArg = callNode.getFirstChild().getNext();
89 |     if (callNode.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.NAME) {
90 |       if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
91 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Support replace this with a value.
92 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(cArg);
93 |         Preconditions.checkState(cArg.getType() == Token.THIS);
94 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
95 |       } else {
96 |         Preconditions.checkState(!NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectApply(callNode));
97 |       }
98 |     }
99 | 
100 |     for (Node fnArg : NodeUtil.getFnParameters(fnNode).children()) {
101 |       if (cArg != null) {
102 |         argMap.put(fnArg.getString(), cArg);
103 |         cArg = cArg.getNext();
104 |       } else {
105 |         Node srcLocation = callNode;
106 |         argMap.put(fnArg.getString(), NodeUtil.newUndefinedNode(srcLocation));
107 |       }
108 |     }
109 | 
110 |     // Add temp names for arguments that don't have named parameters in the
111 |     // called function.
112 |     int anonArg = 0;
113 |     while (cArg != null) {
114 |       String uniquePlaceholder =
115 |         getUniqueAnonymousParameterName(safeNameIdSupplier);
116 |       argMap.put(uniquePlaceholder, cArg);
117 |       cArg = cArg.getNext();
118 |     }
119 | 
120 |     return argMap;
121 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.maybeAddReference(NodeTraversal,FunctionState,Node,JSModule)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 480-505
Documentation:

Method code:
480 |     void maybeAddReference(NodeTraversal t, FunctionState fs,
481 |         Node callNode, JSModule module) {
482 |       if (!fs.canInline()) {
483 |         return;
484 |       }
485 | 
486 |       boolean referenceAdded = false;
487 |       InliningMode mode = fs.canInlineDirectly()
488 |            ? InliningMode.DIRECT : InliningMode.BLOCK;
489 |       referenceAdded = maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
490 |           t, fs, callNode, module, mode);
491 |       if (!referenceAdded &&
492 |           mode == InliningMode.DIRECT && blockFunctionInliningEnabled) {
493 |         // This reference can not be directly inlined, see if
494 |         // block replacement inlining is possible.
495 |         mode = InliningMode.BLOCK;
496 |         referenceAdded = maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
497 |             t, fs, callNode, module, mode);
498 |       }
499 | 
500 |       if (!referenceAdded) {
501 |         // Don't try to remove a function if we can't inline all
502 |         // the references.
503 |         fs.setRemove(false);
504 |       }
505 |     }

   * Sets the prototype.
   * @param prototype the prototype. If this value is {@code null} it will
   *        silently be discarded.
   */

298 |   public boolean setPrototype(FunctionPrototypeType prototype) {
299 |     if (prototype == null) {

===== M0198 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineCostDelta(Node,Set<String>,InliningMode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 808-859
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The difference between the function definition cost and
   *     inline cost.
   */
Method code:
808 |   private static int inlineCostDelta(
809 |       Node fnNode, Set<String> namesToAlias, InliningMode mode) {
810 |     // The part of the function that is never inlined:
811 |     //    "function xx(xx,xx){}" (15 + (param count * 3) -1;
812 |     int paramCount = NodeUtil.getFnParameters(fnNode).getChildCount();
813 |     int commaCount = (paramCount > 1) ? paramCount - 1 : 0;
814 |     int costDeltaFunctionOverhead = 15 + commaCount +
815 |         (paramCount * InlineCostEstimator.ESTIMATED_IDENTIFIER_COST);
816 | 
817 |     Node block = fnNode.getLastChild();
818 |     if (!block.hasChildren()) {
819 |       // Assume the inline cost is zero for empty functions.
820 |       return -costDeltaFunctionOverhead;
821 |     }
822 | 
823 |     if (mode == InliningMode.DIRECT) {
824 |       // The part of the function that is inlined using direct inlining:
825 |       //    "return " (7)
826 |       return -(costDeltaFunctionOverhead + 7);
827 |     } else {
828 |       int aliasCount = namesToAlias.size();
829 | 
830 |       // Originally, we estimated purely base on the function code size, relying
831 |       // on later optimizations. But that did not produce good results, so here
832 |       // we try to estimate the something closer to the actual inlined coded.
833 | 
834 |       // NOTE 1: Result overhead is only if there is an assignment, but
835 |       // getting that information would require some refactoring.
836 |       // NOTE 2: The aliasing overhead is currently an under-estimate,
837 |       // as some parameters are aliased because of the parameters used.
838 |       // Perhaps we should just assume all parameters will be aliased?
839 |       final int INLINE_BLOCK_OVERHEAD = 4; // "X:{}"
840 |       final int PER_RETURN_OVERHEAD = 2;   // "return" --> "break X"
841 |       final int PER_RETURN_RESULT_OVERHEAD = 3; // "XX="
842 |       final int PER_ALIAS_OVERHEAD = 3; // "XX="
843 | 
844 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Counting the number of returns is relatively expensive
845 |       //   this information should be determined during the traversal and
846 |       //   cached.
847 |       int returnCount = NodeUtil.getNodeTypeReferenceCount(
848 |           block, Token.RETURN, new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
849 |       int resultCount = (returnCount > 0) ? returnCount - 1 : 0;
850 |       int baseOverhead = (returnCount > 0) ? INLINE_BLOCK_OVERHEAD : 0;
851 | 
852 |       int overhead = baseOverhead
853 |           + returnCount * PER_RETURN_OVERHEAD
854 |           + resultCount * PER_RETURN_RESULT_OVERHEAD
855 |           + aliasCount * PER_ALIAS_OVERHEAD;
856 | 
857 |       return (overhead - costDeltaFunctionOverhead);
858 |     }
859 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodePrinter.java
Original range: 509-512

     * @param prettyPrint If true, pretty printing will be used.
     */

119 |     @Override
120 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
121 |       if (!inExterns && hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
122 |         traversal.report(node, INVALID_NO_SIDE_EFFECT_ANNOTATION);
123 |       }
124 | 
125 |       if (NodeUtil.isGetProp(node)) {
126 |         if (NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent) &&
127 |             hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node)) {
128 |           noSideEffectFunctionNames.add(node);
129 |         }
130 |       } else if (NodeUtil.isFunction(node)) {
131 | 
132 |         // The annotation may attached to the function node, the
133 |         // variable declaration or assignment expression.
134 |         boolean hasAnnotation = hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(node);
135 |         List<Node> nameNodes = Lists.newArrayList();
136 |         nameNodes.add(node.getFirstChild());
137 | 
138 |         Node nameNode = null;
139 | 
140 |         if (NodeUtil.isName(parent)) {
141 |           Node gramp = parent.getParent();
142 |           if (NodeUtil.isVar(gramp) &&
143 |               gramp.hasOneChild() &&
144 |               hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(gramp)) {
145 |             hasAnnotation = true;
146 |           }
147 | 
148 |           nameNodes.add(parent);
149 |         } else if (NodeUtil.isAssign(parent)) {
150 |           if (hasNoSideEffectsAnnotation(parent)) {
151 |             hasAnnotation = true;
152 |           }
153 | 
154 |           nameNodes.add(parent.getFirstChild());
155 |         }
156 | 
157 |         if (hasAnnotation) {
158 |           noSideEffectFunctionNames.addAll(nameNodes);
159 |         }
160 |       }
161 |     }

===== M0209 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.getCount(Node,Predicate<Node>,Predicate<Node>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2671-2686
Documentation:
/**
   * @return The number of times the the predicate is true for the node
   * or any of its children.
   */
Method code:
2671 |   static int getCount(
2672 |       Node n, Predicate<Node> pred, Predicate<Node> traverseChildrenPred) {
2673 |     int total = 0;
2674 | 
2675 |     if (pred.apply(n)) {
2676 |       total++;
2677 |     }
2678 | 
2679 |     if (traverseChildrenPred.apply(n)) {
2680 |       for (Node c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = c.getNext()) {
2681 |         total += getCount(c, pred, traverseChildrenPred);
2682 |       }
2683 |     }
2684 | 
2685 |     return total;
2686 |   }
