===== M0214 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.VerifyConstants.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 231-280
Documentation:

Method code:
231 |     @Override
232 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
233 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
234 |         String name = n.getString();
235 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
236 |           return;
237 |         }
238 | 
239 |         boolean isConst = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
240 |         if (checkUserDeclarations) {
241 |           boolean expectedConst = false;
242 |           CodingConvention convention = compiler.getCodingConvention();
243 |           if (NodeUtil.isConstantName(n)
244 |               || NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(convention, n, parent)) {
245 |             expectedConst = true;
246 |           } else {
247 |             expectedConst = false;
248 | 
249 |             JSDocInfo info = null;
250 |             Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
251 |             if (var != null) {
252 |               info = var.getJSDocInfo();
253 |             }
254 | 
255 |             if (info != null && info.isConstant()) {
256 |               expectedConst = true;
257 |             } else {
258 |               expectedConst = false;
259 |             }
260 |           }
261 | 
262 |           if (expectedConst) {
263 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
264 |                 "The name " + name + " is not annotated as constant.");
265 |           } else {
266 |             Preconditions.checkState(expectedConst == isConst,
267 |                 "The name " + name + " should not be annotated as constant.");
268 |           }
269 |         }
270 | 
271 |         Boolean value = constantMap.get(name);
272 |         if (value == null) {
273 |           constantMap.put(name, isConst);
274 |         } else {
275 |           Preconditions.checkState(value.booleanValue() == isConst,
276 |               "The name " + name + " is not consistently annotated as " +
277 |               "constant.");
278 |         }
279 |       }
280 |     }

2822 |   static JSDocInfo getFunctionInfo(Node n) {
2823 |     Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);

===== M0236 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MarkNoSideEffectCalls.definitionTypeContainsFunctionType(Definition)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/MarkNoSideEffectCalls.java
Original range: 77-98
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines if the type of the value of the rhs expression can
   * be a function node.
   */
Method code:
77 |   private static boolean definitionTypeContainsFunctionType(Definition def) {
78 |     Node rhs = def.getRValue();
79 |     if (rhs == null) {
80 |       return true;
81 |     }
82 | 
83 |     switch (rhs.getType()) {
84 |       case Token.ASSIGN:
85 |       case Token.AND:
86 |       case Token.CALL:
87 |       case Token.GETPROP:
88 |       case Token.GETELEM:
89 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
90 |       case Token.HOOK:
91 |       case Token.NAME:
92 |       case Token.NEW:
93 |       case Token.OR:
94 |         return true;
95 |       default:
96 |         return false;
97 |     }
98 |   }

===== M0237 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(NodeTraversal,FunctionState,Node,JSModule,InliningMode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 507-534
Documentation:

Method code:
507 |     private boolean maybeAddReferenceUsingMode(
508 |         NodeTraversal t, FunctionState fs, Node callNode,
509 |         JSModule module, InliningMode mode) {
510 | 
511 |       if (specializationState != null) {
512 |         // If we're specializing, make sure we can fixup
513 |         // the containing function before inlining
514 |         Node containingFunction = getContainingFunction(t);
515 |         if (containingFunction != null && !specializationState.canFixupFunction(
516 |             containingFunction)) {
517 |             return false;
518 |         }
519 |       }
520 | 
521 |       CanInlineResult result = injector.canInlineReferenceToFunction(
522 |           t, callNode, fs.getFn().getFunctionNode(),
523 |           fs.getNamesToAlias(), mode, fs.getReferencesThis(),
524 |           fs.hasInnerFunctions());
525 |       if (result != CanInlineResult.NO) {
526 |         // Yeah!
527 |         boolean decompose =
528 |           (result == CanInlineResult.AFTER_DECOMPOSITION);
529 |         fs.addReference(new Reference(callNode, module, mode, decompose));
530 |         return true;
531 |       }
532 | 
533 |       return false;
534 |     }

===== M0243 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.trimCanidatesUsingOnCost()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 654-671
Documentation:
/**
   * Remove entries from the list of candidates that can't be inlined.
   */
Method code:
654 |   void trimCanidatesUsingOnCost() {
655 |     Iterator<Entry<String, FunctionState>> i;
656 |     for (i = fns.entrySet().iterator(); i.hasNext();) {
657 |       FunctionState fs = i.next().getValue();
658 |       if (fs.hasReferences()) {
659 |         // Only inline function if it decreases the code size.
660 |         boolean lowersCost = mimimizeCost(fs);
661 |         if (!lowersCost) {
662 |           // It shouldn't be inlined; remove it from the list.
663 |           i.remove();
664 |         }
665 |       } else if (!fs.canRemove()) {
666 |         // Don't bother tracking functions without references that can't be
667 |         // removed.
668 |         i.remove();
669 |       }
670 |     }
671 |   }

   * @return Whether the specified node has a loop parent that

1588 |   static boolean isWithinLoop(Node n) {

1590 |       if (NodeUtil.isLoopStructure(parent)) {
1591 |         return true;

===== M0259 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeTraversal.traverseBranch(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeTraversal.java
Original range: 437-465
Documentation:
/**
   * Traverses a branch.
   */
Method code:
437 |   @SuppressWarnings("fallthrough")
438 |   private void traverseBranch(Node n, Node parent) {
439 |     int type = n.getType();
440 |     if (type == Token.SCRIPT) {
441 |       sourceName = getSourceName(n);
442 |     }
443 | 
444 |     curNode = n;
445 |     if (!callback.shouldTraverse(this, n, parent)) return;
446 | 
447 |     switch (type) {
448 |       case Token.FUNCTION:
449 |         traverseFunction(n, parent);
450 |         break;
451 | 
452 |       default:
453 |         for (Node child = n.getFirstChild(); child != null; ) {
454 |           // child could be replaced, in which case our child node
455 |           // would no longer point to the true next
456 |           Node next = child.getNext();
457 |           traverseBranch(child, n);
458 |           child = next;
459 |         }
460 |         break;
461 |     }
462 | 
463 |     curNode = n;
464 |     callback.visit(this, n, parent);
465 |   }

===== M0265 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inliningLowersCost(JSModule,Node,Collection<? extends Reference>,Set<String>,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 688-744
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine if inlining the function is likely to reduce the code size.
   * @param namesToAlias
   */
Method code:
688 |   boolean inliningLowersCost(
689 |       JSModule fnModule, Node fnNode, Collection<? extends Reference> refs,
690 |       Set<String> namesToAlias, boolean isRemovable, boolean referencesThis) {
691 |     int referenceCount = refs.size();
692 |     if (referenceCount == 0) {
693 |       return true;
694 |     }
695 | 
696 |     int referencesUsingBlockInlining = 0;
697 | 
698 |     boolean checkModules = isRemovable && fnModule != null;
699 |     JSModuleGraph moduleGraph = compiler.getModuleGraph();
700 | 
701 |     for (Reference ref : refs) {
702 |       if (ref.mode == InliningMode.BLOCK) {
703 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining++;
704 |       }
705 | 
706 |       // Check if any of the references cross the module boundaries.
707 |       if (checkModules && ref.module != null) {
708 |         if (ref.module != fnModule &&
709 |             !moduleGraph.dependsOn(ref.module, fnModule)) {
710 |           // Calculate the cost as if the function were non-removable,
711 |           // if it still lowers the cost inline it.
712 |           isRemovable = false;
713 |           checkModules = false;  // no need to check additional modules.
714 |         }
715 |       }
716 |     }
717 | 
718 |     int referencesUsingDirectInlining = referenceCount -
719 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining;
720 | 
721 |     // Don't bother calculating the cost of function for simple functions where
722 |     // possible.
723 |     // However, when inlining a complex function, even a single reference may be
724 |     // larger than the original function if there are many returns (resulting
725 |     // in additional assignments) or many parameters that need to be aliased
726 |     // so use the cost estimating.
727 |     if (referenceCount == 1 && isRemovable &&
728 |         referencesUsingDirectInlining == 1) {
729 |       return true;
730 |     }
731 | 
732 |     int callCost = estimateCallCost(fnNode, referencesThis);
733 |     int overallCallCost = callCost * referenceCount;
734 | 
735 |     int costDeltaDirect = inlineCostDelta(
736 |         fnNode, namesToAlias, InliningMode.DIRECT);
737 |     int costDeltaBlock = inlineCostDelta(
738 |         fnNode, namesToAlias, InliningMode.BLOCK);
739 | 
740 |     return doesLowerCost(fnNode, overallCallCost,
741 |         referencesUsingDirectInlining, costDeltaDirect,
742 |         referencesUsingBlockInlining, costDeltaBlock,
743 |         isRemovable);
744 |   }

===== M0275 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(Node,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/DefinitionsRemover.java
Original range: 36-64
Documentation:
/**
   * @return an {@link Definition} object if the node contains a definition or
   *     {@code null} otherwise.
   */
Method code:
36 |   static Definition getDefinition(Node n, boolean isExtern) {
37 |     // TODO(user): Since we have parent pointers handy. A lot of constructors
38 |     // can be simplified.
39 | 
40 |     // This logic must match #isDefinitionNode
41 |     Node parent = n.getParent();
42 |     if (parent == null) {
43 |       return null;
44 |     }
45 | 
46 |     if (NodeUtil.isVarDeclaration(n) && n.hasChildren()) {
47 |       return new VarDefinition(n, isExtern);
48 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isFunction(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
49 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(parent)) {
50 |         return new NamedFunctionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
51 |       } else if (!n.getString().equals("")) {
52 |         return new FunctionExpressionDefinition(parent, isExtern);
53 |       }
54 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isAssign(parent) && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
55 |       return new AssignmentDefinition(parent, isExtern);
56 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent)) {
57 |       return new ObjectLiteralPropertyDefinition(parent, n, n.getFirstChild(),
58 |           isExtern);
59 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LP) {
60 |       Node function = parent.getParent();
61 |       return new FunctionArgumentDefinition(function, n, isExtern);
62 |     }
63 |     return null;
64 |   }

===== M0280 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.CallVisitor.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 380-411
Documentation:

Method code:
380 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
381 |       switch (n.getType()) {
382 |         // Function calls
383 |         case Token.CALL:
384 |           Node child = n.getFirstChild();
385 |           String name = null;
386 |           // NOTE: The normalization pass insures that local names do not
387 |           // collide with global names.
388 |           if (child.getType() == Token.NAME) {
389 |             name = child.getString();
390 |           } else if (child.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
391 |             name = anonFunctionMap.get(child);
392 |           } else if (NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(n)) {
393 |             Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isGet(child));
394 |             Node fnIdentifingNode = child.getFirstChild();
395 |             if (fnIdentifingNode.getType() == Token.NAME) {
396 |               name = fnIdentifingNode.getString();
397 |             } else if (fnIdentifingNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
398 |               name = anonFunctionMap.get(fnIdentifingNode);
399 |             }
400 |           }
401 | 
402 |           if (name != null) {
403 |             FunctionState fs = functionMap.get(name);
404 |             // Only visit call-sites for functions that can be inlined.
405 |             if (fs != null) {
406 |               callback.visitCallSite(t, n, parent, fs);
407 |             }
408 |           }
409 |           break;
410 |       }
411 |     }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Tracer.Tracer(String,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Tracer.java
Original range: 234-277
Documentation:
/**
   * Create and start a tracer.
   * Both type and comment may be null. See class comment for usage.
   *
   * @param type The type for totalling
   * @param comment Comment about this tracer
   */
Method code:
234 |   Tracer(@Nullable String type, @Nullable String comment) {
235 |     this.type = type;
236 |     this.comment = comment == null ? "" : comment;
237 |     startTimeMs = clock.currentTimeMillis();
238 |     startThread = Thread.currentThread();
239 |     if (!extraTracingStatistics.isEmpty()) {
240 |       int size = extraTracingStatistics.size();
241 |       extraTracingValues = new long[size];
242 |       int i = 0;
243 |       for (TracingStatistic tracingStatistic : extraTracingStatistics) {
244 |         extraTracingValues[i] = tracingStatistic.start(startThread);
245 |         i++;
246 |       }
247 |     }
248 | 
249 |     ThreadTrace trace = getThreadTrace();
250 | 
251 |     // Do nothing if the current thread trace wasn't initialized.
252 |     if (!trace.isInitialized()) {
253 |       return;
254 |     }
255 | 
256 |     // Check if we are creating too many Tracers.
257 |     if (trace.events.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
258 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
259 |                   "Giant thread trace. Too many Tracers created. "
260 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
261 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
262 |       trace.truncateEvents();
263 |     }
264 | 
265 |     // Check if we forgot to close the Tracers.
266 |     if (trace.outstandingEvents.size() >= MAX_TRACE_SIZE) {
267 |       logger.log(Level.WARNING,
268 |                   "Too many outstanding Tracers. Tracer.stop() is missing "
269 |                     + "or Tracer.stop() is not wrapped in a "
270 |                     + "try/finally block. "
271 |                     + "Clearing to avoid memory leak.",
272 |                   new Throwable(trace.toString()));
273 |       trace.truncateOutstandingEvents();
274 |     }
275 | 
276 |     trace.startEvent(this);
277 |   }

===== M0296 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.Inline.visitCallSite(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,FunctionState)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 594-620
Documentation:

Method code:
594 |     public void visitCallSite(
595 |         NodeTraversal t, Node callNode, Node parent, FunctionState fs) {
596 |       Preconditions.checkState(fs.hasExistingFunctionDefinition());
597 |       if (fs.canInline()) {
598 |         Reference ref = fs.getReference(callNode);
599 |         // There are two cases ref can be null: if the call site was introduce
600 |         // because it was part of a function that was inlined during this pass
601 |         // or if the call site was trimmed from the list of references because
602 |         // the function couldn't be inlined at this location.
603 |         if (ref != null) {
604 |           if (specializationState != null) {
605 |             Node containingFunction = getContainingFunction(t);
606 | 
607 |             if (containingFunction != null) {
608 |               // Report that the function was specialized so that
609 |               // {@link SpecializeModule} can fix it up.
610 |               specializationState.reportSpecializedFunction(containingFunction);
611 |             }
612 |           }
613 | 
614 |           inlineFunction(t, callNode, fs, ref.mode);
615 |           // Keep track of references that have been inlined so that
616 |           // we can verify that none have been missed.
617 |           ref.inlined = true;
618 |         }
619 |       }
620 |     }

===== M0319 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.resolveInlineConflicts()
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 724-728
Documentation:
/**
   * Size base inlining calculations are thrown off when a function that is
   * being inlined also contains calls to functions that are slated for
   * inlining.
   *
   * Specifically, a clone of the FUNCTION node tree is used when the function
   * is inlined. Calls in this new tree are not included in the list of function
   * references so they won't be inlined (which is what we want). Here we mark
   * those functions as non-removable (as they will have new references in the
   * cloned node trees).
   *
   * This prevents a function that would only be inlined because it is
   * referenced once from being inlined into multiple call sites because
   * the calling function has been inlined in multiple locations or the
   * function being removed while there are still references.
   */
Method code:
724 |   private void resolveInlineConflicts() {
725 |     for (FunctionState fs : fns.values()) {
726 |       resolveInlineConflictsForFunction(fs);
727 |     }
728 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 389-395
Documentation:
/**
     * Rewrite named unhoisted functions declarations to a known
     * consistent behavior so we don't to different logic paths for the same
     * code. From:
     *    function f() {}
     * to:
     *    var f = function () {};
     */
Method code:
389 |     private void normalizeFunctionDeclaration(Node n) {
390 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION);
391 |       if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)
392 |           && !NodeUtil.isHoistedFunctionDeclaration(n)) {
393 |         rewriteFunctionDeclaration(n);
394 |       }
395 |     }

Method code:
773 |   private void validateMinimumChildCount(Node n, int i) {

776 |       valid = n.hasChildren();
777 |     } else if (i == 2) {
778 |       valid = n.hasMoreThanOneChild();
779 |     } else {
780 |       valid = n.getChildCount() >= i;
781 |     }
782 | 
783 |     if (!valid) {
784 |       violation(
785 |           "Expected at least " + i + " children, but was "
786 |               + n.getChildCount(), n);
787 |     }
788 |   }

===== M0332 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 527-549
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * There are several criteria that the function and reference must hold in
   * order for the functions to be inlined:
   * - It must be a simple call, or assignment, or var initialization.
   * <pre>
   *    f();
   *    a = foo();
   *    var a = foo();
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
527 |   private CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(
528 |       NodeTraversal t, Node callNode, Node fnNode, Set<String> namesToAlias) {
529 |     CallSiteType callSiteType = classifyCallSite(callNode);
530 |     if (callSiteType == CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED) {
531 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
532 |     }
533 | 
534 |     if (!allowDecomposition
535 |         && callSiteType == CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION) {
536 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
537 |     }
538 | 
539 |     if (!callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(
540 |             t, callNode, fnNode, namesToAlias)) {
541 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
542 |     }
543 | 
544 |     if (callSiteType == CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION)  {
545 |       return CanInlineResult.AFTER_DECOMPOSITION;
546 |     } else {
547 |       return CanInlineResult.YES;
548 |     }
549 |   }

Original range: 175-191
Documentation:
/**
     * There are two types of calls we are interested in calls without explicit
     * "this" values (what we are call "free" calls) and direct call to eval.
     */
Method code:
175 |     private void annotateCalls(Node n) {
176 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.CALL);
177 | 
178 |       // Keep track of of the "this" context of a call.  A call without an
179 |       // explicit "this" is a free call.
180 |       Node first = n.getFirstChild();
181 |       if (!NodeUtil.isGet(first)) {
182 |         n.putBooleanProp(Node.FREE_CALL, true);
183 |       }
184 | 
185 |       // Keep track of the context in which eval is called. It is important
186 |       // to distinguish between "(0, eval)()" and "eval()".
187 |       if (first.getType() == Token.NAME &&
188 |           "eval".equals(first.getString())) {
189 |         first.putBooleanProp(Node.DIRECT_EVAL, true);
190 |       }

===== M0342 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.GoogleCodingConvention.isConstant(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/GoogleCodingConvention.java
Original range: 60-77
Documentation:
/**
   * {@inheritDoc}
   *
   * <p>This enforces the Google const name convention, that the first character
   * after the last $ must be an upper-case letter and all subsequent letters
   * must be upper case. The name must be at least 2 characters long.
   *
   * <p>Examples:
   * <pre>
   *      aaa          Not constant - lower-case letters in the name
   *      A            Not constant - too short
   *      goog$A       Constant - letters after the $ are upper-case.
   *      AA17         Constant - digits can appear after the first letter
   *      goog$7A      Not constant - first character after the $ must be
   *                   upper case.
   *      $A           Constant - doesn't have to be anything in front of the $
   * </pre>
   */
Method code:
60 |   @Override
61 |   public boolean isConstant(String name) {
62 |     if (name.length() <= 1) {
63 |       return false;
64 |     }
65 | 
66 |     // In compiled code, '$' is often a namespace delimiter. To allow inlining
67 |     // of namespaced constants, we strip off any namespaces here.
68 |     int pos = name.lastIndexOf('$');
69 |     if (pos >= 0) {
70 |       name = name.substring(pos + 1);
71 |       if (name.length() == 0) {
72 |         return false;
73 |       }
74 |     }
75 | 
76 |     return isConstantKey(name);
77 |   }

773 |   private PropListItem removeProp(PropListItem item, int propType) {

===== M0351 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSTypeRegistry.registerPropertyOnType(String,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSTypeRegistry.java
Original range: 607-619
Documentation:
/**
   * Tells the type system that {@code owner} may have a property named
   * {@code propertyName}. This allows the registry to keep track of what
   * types a property is defined upon.
   *
   * This is NOT the same as saying that {@code owner} must have a property
   * named type. ObjectType#hasProperty attempts to minimize false positives
   * ("if we're not sure, then don't type check this property"). The type
   * registry, on the other hand, should attempt to minimize false negatives
   * ("if this property is assigned anywhere in the program, it must
   * show up in the type registry").
   */
Method code:
607 |   public void registerPropertyOnType(String propertyName, JSType type) {
608 |     UnionTypeBuilder typeSet = typesIndexedByProperty.get(propertyName);
609 |     if (typeSet == null) {
610 |       typeSet = new UnionTypeBuilder(this);
611 |       typesIndexedByProperty.put(propertyName, typeSet);
612 |     }
613 | 
614 |     typeSet.addAlternate(type);
615 |     addReferenceTypeIndexedByProperty(propertyName, type);
616 | 
617 |     // Clear cached values that depend on typesIndexedByProperty.
618 |     greatestSubtypeByProperty.remove(propertyName);
619 |   }

===== M0359 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.doesLowerCost(Node,int,int,int,int,int,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 749-777
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether inlining will lower cost.
   */
Method code:
749 |   private boolean doesLowerCost(
750 |       Node fnNode, int callCost,
751 |       int directInlines, int costDeltaDirect,
752 |       int blockInlines, int costDeltaBlock,
753 |       boolean removable) {
754 | 
755 |     // Determine the threshold value for this inequality:
756 |     //     inline_cost < call_cost
757 |     // But solve it for the function declaration size so the size of it
758 |     // is only calculated once and terminated early if possible.
759 | 
760 |     int fnInstanceCount = directInlines + blockInlines - (removable ? 1 : 0);
761 |     // Prevent division by zero.
762 |     if (fnInstanceCount == 0) {
763 |       // Special case single reference function that are being block inlined:
764 |       // If the cost of the inline is greater than the function definition size,
765 |       // don't inline.
766 |       if (blockInlines > 0 && costDeltaBlock > 0) {
767 |         return false;
768 |       }
769 |       return true;
770 |     }
771 | 
772 |     int costDelta = (directInlines * costDeltaDirect) +
773 |         (blockInlines * costDeltaBlock);
774 |     int threshold = (callCost - costDelta) / fnInstanceCount;
775 | 
776 |     return InlineCostEstimator.getCost(fnNode, threshold + 1) <= threshold;
777 |   }

===== M0364 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.canInlineReferenceToFunction(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>,InliningMode,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 156-190
Documentation:
/**
   * @param t  The traversal use to reach the call site.
   * @param callNode The CALL node.
   * @param fnNode The function to evaluate for inlining.
   * @param needAliases A set of function parameter names that can not be
   *     used without aliasing. Returned by getUnsafeParameterNames().
   * @param mode Inlining mode to be used.
   * @param referencesThis Whether fnNode contains references to its this
   *     object.
   * @param containsFunctions Whether fnNode contains inner functions.
   * @return Whether the inlining can occur.
   */
Method code:
156 |   CanInlineResult canInlineReferenceToFunction(NodeTraversal t,
157 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode, Set<String> needAliases,
158 |       InliningMode mode, boolean referencesThis, boolean containsFunctions) {
159 |     // TODO(johnlenz): This function takes too many parameter, without
160 |     // context.  Modify the API to take a structure describing the function.
161 | 
162 |     // Allow direct function calls or "fn.call" style calls.
163 |     if (!isSupportedCallType(callNode)) {
164 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
165 |     }
166 | 
167 |     // Limit where functions that contain functions can be inline.  Introducing
168 |     // an inner function into another function can capture a variable and cause
169 |     // a memory leak.  This isn't a problem in the global scope as those values
170 |     // last until explicitly cleared.
171 |     if (containsFunctions && !t.inGlobalScope()) {
172 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Allow inlining into any scope without local names or
173 |       // inner functions.
174 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
175 |     }
176 | 
177 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Add support for 'apply'
178 |     if (referencesThis && !NodeUtil.isFunctionObjectCall(callNode)) {
179 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Allow 'this' references to be replaced with a
180 |       // global 'this' object.
181 |       return CanInlineResult.NO;
182 |     }
183 | 
184 |     if (mode == InliningMode.DIRECT) {
185 |       return canInlineReferenceDirectly(callNode, fnNode);
186 |     } else {
187 |       return canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock(
188 |           t, callNode, fnNode, needAliases);
189 |     }
190 |   }

===== M0371 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionParamBuilder.addVarArgs(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionParamBuilder.java
Original range: 94-109
Documentation:
/**
   * Add variable arguments to the end of the parameter list.
   * @return False if this is called after var args are added.
   */
Method code:
94 |   public boolean addVarArgs(JSType type) {
95 |     if (hasVarArgs()) {
96 |       return false;
97 |     }
98 | 
99 |     // There are two types of variable argument functions:
100 |     // 1) Programmer-defined var args
101 |     // 2) Native bottom types that can accept any argument.
102 |     // For the first one, "undefined" is a valid value for all arguments.
103 |     // For the second, we do not want to cast it up to undefined.
104 |     if (!type.isEmptyType()) {
105 |       type = registry.createOptionalType(type);
106 |     }
107 |     newParameter(type).setVarArgs(true);
108 |     return true;
109 |   }

===== M0380 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 317-345
Documentation:

Method code:
317 |     @Override
318 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
319 |       switch (n.getType()) {
320 |         case Token.WHILE:
321 |           if (CONVERT_WHILE_TO_FOR) {
322 |             Node expr = n.getFirstChild();
323 |             n.setType(Token.FOR);
324 |             Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
325 |             empty.copyInformationFrom(n);
326 |             n.addChildBefore(empty, expr);
327 |             n.addChildAfter(empty.cloneNode(), expr);
328 |             reportCodeChange("WHILE node");
329 |           }
330 |           break;
331 | 
332 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
333 |           normalizeFunctionDeclaration(n);
334 |           break;
335 | 
336 |         case Token.NAME:
337 |         case Token.STRING:
338 |         case Token.GET:
339 |         case Token.SET:
340 |           if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalizedObfuscated()) {
341 |             annotateConstantsByConvention(n, parent);
342 |           }
343 |           break;
344 |       }
345 |     }

===== M0394 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SyntacticScopeCreator.scanRoot(Node,Scope)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SyntacticScopeCreator.java
Original range: 82-112
Documentation:

Method code:
82 |   private void scanRoot(Node n, Scope parent) {
83 |     if (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
84 |       sourceName = (String) n.getProp(Node.SOURCENAME_PROP);
85 | 
86 |       final Node fnNameNode = n.getFirstChild();
87 |       final Node args = fnNameNode.getNext();
88 |       final Node body = args.getNext();
89 | 
90 |       // Bleed the function name into the scope, if it hasn't
91 |       // been declared in the outer scope.
92 |       String fnName = fnNameNode.getString();
93 |       if (!fnName.isEmpty() && NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
94 |         declareVar(fnNameNode);
95 |       }
96 | 
97 |       // Args: Declare function variables
98 |       Preconditions.checkState(args.getType() == Token.LP);
99 |       for (Node a = args.getFirstChild(); a != null;
100 |            a = a.getNext()) {
101 |         Preconditions.checkState(a.getType() == Token.NAME);
102 |         declareVar(a);
103 |       }
104 | 
105 |       // Body
106 |       scanVars(body, n);
107 |     } else {
108 |       // It's the global block
109 |       Preconditions.checkState(scope.getParent() == null);
110 |       scanVars(n, null);
111 |     }
112 |   }

===== M0402 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 171-202
Documentation:

Method code:
171 |     @Override
172 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
173 |       // Note: Constant properties annotations are not propagated.
174 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
175 |         if (n.getString().isEmpty()) {
176 |           return;
177 |         }
178 | 
179 |         JSDocInfo info = null;
180 |         // Find the JSDocInfo for a top level variable.
181 |         Var var = t.getScope().getVar(n.getString());
182 |         if (var != null) {
183 |           info = var.getJSDocInfo();
184 |         }
185 | 
186 |         boolean shouldBeConstant =
187 |             (info != null && info.isConstant()) ||
188 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
189 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent);
190 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
191 |         if (shouldBeConstant && !isMarkedConstant) {
192 |           if (assertOnChange) {
193 |             String name = n.getString();
194 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
195 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
196 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
197 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
198 |           }
199 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
200 |         }
201 |       }
202 |     }

===== M0403 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.ScriptRuntime.getMessage(String,Object[])
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/ScriptRuntime.java
Original range: 456-483
Documentation:
/* OPT there's a noticable delay for the first error!  Maybe it'd
     * make sense to use a ListResourceBundle instead of a properties
     * file to avoid (synchronized) text parsing.
     */
Method code:
456 |     public static String getMessage(String messageId, Object[] arguments)
457 |     {
458 |         final String defaultResource
459 |             = "rhino_ast.java.com.google.javascript.rhino.Messages";
460 | 
461 |         Context cx = Context.getCurrentContext();
462 |         Locale locale = cx != null ? cx.getLocale() : Locale.getDefault();
463 | 
464 |         // ResourceBundle does cacheing.
465 |         ResourceBundle rb = ResourceBundle.getBundle(defaultResource, locale);
466 | 
467 |         String formatString;
468 |         try {
469 |             formatString = rb.getString(messageId);
470 |         } catch (java.util.MissingResourceException mre) {
471 |             throw new RuntimeException
472 |                 ("no message resource found for message property "+ messageId);
473 |         }
474 | 
475 |         /*
476 |          * It's OK to format the string, even if 'arguments' is null;
477 |          * we need to format it anyway, to make double ''s collapse to
478 |          * single 's.
479 |          */
480 |         // TODO: MessageFormat is not available on pJava
481 |         MessageFormat formatter = new MessageFormat(formatString);
482 |         return formatter.format(arguments);
483 |     }

===== M0407 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.classifyCallSite(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 330-370
Documentation:
/**
   * Determine which, if any, of the supported types the call site is.
   */
Method code:
330 |   private CallSiteType classifyCallSite(Node callNode) {
331 |     Node parent = callNode.getParent();
332 |     Node grandParent = parent.getParent();
333 | 
334 |     // Verify the call site:
335 |     if (NodeUtil.isExprCall(parent)) {
336 |       // This is a simple call?  Example: "foo();".
337 |       return CallSiteType.SIMPLE_CALL;
338 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isExprAssign(grandParent)
339 |         && !NodeUtil.isLhs(callNode, parent)
340 |         && parent.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.NAME
341 |         && !NodeUtil.isConstantName(parent.getFirstChild())) {
342 |       // This is a simple assignment.  Example: "x = foo();"
343 |       return CallSiteType.SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT;
344 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.NAME
345 |         && !NodeUtil.isConstantName(parent)
346 |         && grandParent.getType() == Token.VAR
347 |         && grandParent.hasOneChild()) {
348 |       // This is a var declaration.  Example: "var x = foo();"
349 |       // TODO(johnlenz): Should we be checking for constants on the
350 |       // left-hand-side of the assignments (and handling them as EXPRESSION?
351 |       return CallSiteType.VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT;
352 |     } else {
353 |       Node expressionRoot = ExpressionDecomposer.findExpressionRoot(callNode);
354 |       if (expressionRoot != null) {
355 |         ExpressionDecomposer decomposer = new ExpressionDecomposer(
356 |             compiler, safeNameIdSupplier, knownConstants);
357 |         DecompositionType type = decomposer.canExposeExpression(
358 |             callNode);
359 |         if (type == DecompositionType.MOVABLE) {
360 |           return CallSiteType.EXPRESSION;
361 |         } else if (type == DecompositionType.DECOMPOSABLE) {
362 |           return CallSiteType.DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION;
363 |         } else {
364 |           Preconditions.checkState(type == DecompositionType.UNDECOMPOSABLE);
365 |         }
366 |       }
367 |     }
368 | 
369 |     return CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED;
370 |   }

===== M0414 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isSubtype(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 746-788
Documentation:
/**
   * A function is a subtype of another if their call methods are related via
   * subtyping and {@code this} is a subtype of {@code that} with regard to
   * the prototype chain.
   */
Method code:
746 |   @Override
747 |   public boolean isSubtype(JSType that) {
748 |     if (JSType.isSubtype(this, that)) {
749 |       return true;
750 |     }
751 | 
752 |     if (that.isFunctionType()) {
753 |       if (((FunctionType) that).isInterface()) {
754 |         // Any function can be assigned to an interface function.
755 |         return true;
756 |       }
757 |       if (this.isInterface()) {
758 |         // An interface function cannot be assigned to anything.
759 |         return false;
760 |       }
761 |       // If functionA is a subtype of functionB, then their "this" types
762 |       // should be contravariant. However, this causes problems because
763 |       // of the way we enforce overrides. Because function(this:SubFoo)
764 |       // is not a subtype of function(this:Foo), our override check treats
765 |       // this as an error. It also screws up out standard method
766 |       // for aliasing constructors. Let's punt on all this for now.
767 |       // TODO(nicksantos): fix this.
768 |       FunctionType other = (FunctionType) that;
769 |       boolean treatThisTypesAsCovariant =
770 |         // If either one of these is a ctor, skip 'this' checking.
771 |         this.isConstructor() || other.isConstructor() ||
772 | 
773 |         // An interface 'this'-type is non-restrictive.
774 |         // In practical terms, if C implements I, and I has a method m,
775 |         // then any m doesn't necessarily have to C#m's 'this'
776 |         // type doesn't need to match I.
777 |         (other.typeOfThis.getConstructor() != null &&
778 |              other.typeOfThis.getConstructor().isInterface()) ||
779 | 
780 |         // If one of the 'this' types is covariant of the other,
781 |         // then we'll treat them as covariant (see comment above).
782 |         other.typeOfThis.isSubtype(this.typeOfThis) ||
783 |         this.typeOfThis.isSubtype(other.typeOfThis);
784 |       return treatThisTypesAsCovariant && this.call.isSubtype(other.call);
785 |     }
786 | 
787 |     return getNativeType(JSTypeNative.FUNCTION_PROTOTYPE).isSubtype(that);
788 |   }

===== M0426 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1924-1926
Documentation:
/**
   * Is a FUNCTION node an function expression? An function expression is one
   * that has either no name or a name that is not added to the current scope.
   *
   * <p>Some examples of function expressions:
   * <pre>
   * (function () {})
   * (function f() {})()
   * [ function f() {} ]
   * var f = function f() {};
   * for (function f() {};;) {}
   * </pre>
   *
   * <p>Some examples of functions that are <em>not</em> expressions:
   * <pre>
   * function f() {}
   * if (x); else function f() {}
   * for (;;) { function f() {} }
   * </pre>
   *
   * @param n A node
   * @return Whether n is an function used within an expression.
   */
Method code:
1924 |   static boolean isFunctionExpression(Node n) {
1925 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && !isStatement(n);
1926 |   }

/**

/**
   * Reduce the alternates into a non-union type.
   * If the alternates can't be accurately represented with a non-union
   * type, return null.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private JSType reduceAlternatesWithoutUnion() {
164 |     if (isAllType) {
165 |       return registry.getNativeType(ALL_TYPE);
166 |     } else if (isNativeUnknownType) {
167 |       if (areAllUnknownsChecked) {
168 |         return registry.getNativeType(CHECKED_UNKNOWN_TYPE);
169 |       } else {
170 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
171 |       }
172 |     } else {

174 |       if (size > MAX_UNION_SIZE) {
175 |         return registry.getNativeType(UNKNOWN_TYPE);
176 |       } else if (size > 1) {
177 |         return null;
178 |       } else if (size == 1) {
179 |         return alternates.iterator().next();
180 |       } else {
181 |         return registry.getNativeType(NO_TYPE);
182 |       }
183 |     }
184 |   }

===== M0451 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.inlineFunction(Node,Node,String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 377-484
Documentation:
/**
   * Inline a function which fulfills the requirements of
   * canInlineReferenceAsStatementBlock into the call site, replacing the
   * parent expression.
   */
Method code:
377 |   private Node inlineFunction(
378 |       Node callNode, Node fnNode, String fnName) {
379 |     Node parent = callNode.getParent();
380 |     Node grandParent = parent.getParent();
381 | 
382 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Consider storing the callSite classification in the
383 |     // reference object and passing it in here.
384 |     CallSiteType callSiteType = classifyCallSite(callNode);
385 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(callSiteType != CallSiteType.UNSUPPORTED);
386 | 
387 |     // Store the name for the result. This will be used to
388 |     // replace "return expr" statements with "resultName = expr"
389 |     // to replace
390 |     String resultName = null;
391 |     boolean needsDefaultReturnResult = true;
392 |     switch (callSiteType) {
393 |       case SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
394 |         resultName = parent.getFirstChild().getString();
395 |         break;
396 | 
397 |       case VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
398 |         resultName = parent.getString();
399 |         break;
400 | 
401 |       case SIMPLE_CALL:
402 |         resultName = null;  // "foo()" doesn't need a result.
403 |         needsDefaultReturnResult = false;
404 |         break;
405 | 
406 |       case EXPRESSION:
407 |         resultName = getUniqueResultName();
408 |         needsDefaultReturnResult = false; // The intermediary result already
409 |                                           // has the default value.
410 |         break;
411 | 
412 |       case DECOMPOSABLE_EXPRESSION:
413 |         throw new IllegalStateException(
414 |             "Decomposable expressions must decomposed before inlining.");
415 | 
416 |       default:
417 |         throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected call site type.");
418 |     }
419 | 
420 |     boolean isCallInLoop = NodeUtil.isWithinLoop(callNode);
421 | 
422 |     FunctionToBlockMutator mutator = new FunctionToBlockMutator(
423 |         compiler, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
424 | 
425 |     Node newBlock = mutator.mutate(
426 |         fnName, fnNode, callNode, resultName,
427 |         needsDefaultReturnResult, isCallInLoop);
428 | 
429 |     // TODO(nicksantos): Create a common mutation function that
430 |     // can replace either a VAR name assignment, assignment expression or
431 |     // a EXPR_RESULT.
432 |     Node greatGrandParent = grandParent.getParent();
433 |     switch (callSiteType) {
434 |       case VAR_DECL_SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
435 |         // Remove the call from the name node.
436 |         parent.removeChild(parent.getFirstChild());
437 |         Preconditions.checkState(parent.getFirstChild() == null);
438 |         // Add the call, after the VAR.
439 |         greatGrandParent.addChildAfter(newBlock, grandParent);
440 |         break;
441 | 
442 |       case SIMPLE_ASSIGNMENT:
443 |         // The assignment is now part of the inline function so
444 |         // replace it completely.
445 |         Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(grandParent));
446 |         greatGrandParent.replaceChild(grandParent, newBlock);
447 |         break;
448 | 
449 |       case SIMPLE_CALL:
450 |         // If nothing is looking at the result just replace the call.
451 |         Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent));
452 |         grandParent.replaceChild(parent, newBlock);
453 |         break;
454 | 
455 |       case EXPRESSION:
456 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Maybe change this so that movable and decomposable
457 |         // expressions are handled the same way: The call is moved and
458 |         // then handled by one the three basic cases, rather than
459 |         // introducing a new case.
460 |         Node injectionPoint = ExpressionDecomposer.findInjectionPoint(callNode);
461 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(injectionPoint);
462 |         Node injectionPointParent = injectionPoint.getParent();
463 |         Preconditions.checkNotNull(injectionPointParent);
464 |         Preconditions.checkState(
465 |             NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(injectionPointParent));
466 | 
467 |         // Declare the intermediate result name.
468 |         newBlock.addChildrenToFront(
469 |             NodeUtil.newVarNode(resultName, null)
470 |                  .copyInformationFromForTree(callNode));
471 |         // Inline the function before the selected injection point (before
472 |         // the call).
473 |         injectionPointParent.addChildBefore(newBlock, injectionPoint);
474 |         // Replace the call site with a reference to the intermediate
475 |         // result name.
476 |         parent.replaceChild(callNode, Node.newString(Token.NAME, resultName));
477 |         break;
478 | 
479 |       default:
480 |         throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected call site type.");
481 |     }
482 | 
483 |     return newBlock;
484 |   }

===== M0459 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.removeChild(Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 1740-1793
Documentation:
/** Safely remove children while maintaining a valid node structure. */
Method code:
1740 |   static void removeChild(Node parent, Node node) {
1741 |     if (isTryFinallyNode(parent, node)) {
1742 |       if (NodeUtil.hasCatchHandler(getCatchBlock(parent))) {
1743 |         // A finally can only be removed if there is a catch.
1744 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1745 |       } else {
1746 |         // Otherwise only its children can be removed.
1747 |         node.detachChildren();
1748 |       }
1749 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.CATCH) {
1750 |       // The CATCH can can only be removed if there is a finally clause.
1751 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent().getParent();
1752 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1753 |       node.detachFromParent();
1754 |     } else if (isTryCatchNodeContainer(node)) {
1755 |       // The container node itself can't be removed, but the contained CATCH
1756 |       // can if there is a 'finally' clause
1757 |       Node tryNode = node.getParent();
1758 |       Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.hasFinally(tryNode));
1759 |       node.detachChildren();
1760 |     } else if (node.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1761 |       // Simply empty the block.  This maintains source location and
1762 |       // "synthetic"-ness.
1763 |       node.detachChildren();
1764 |     } else if (isStatementBlock(parent)
1765 |         || isSwitchCase(node)) {
1766 |       // A statement in a block can simply be removed.
1767 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1768 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR) {
1769 |       if (parent.hasMoreThanOneChild()) {
1770 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1771 |       } else {
1772 |         // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1773 |         parent.removeChild(node);
1774 |         // This would leave an empty VAR, remove the VAR itself.
1775 |         removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1776 |       }
1777 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.LABEL
1778 |         && node == parent.getLastChild()) {
1779 |       // Remove the node from the parent, so it can be reused.
1780 |       parent.removeChild(node);
1781 |       // A LABEL without children can not be referred to, remove it.
1782 |       removeChild(parent.getParent(), parent);
1783 |     } else if (parent.getType() == Token.FOR
1784 |         && parent.getChildCount() == 4) {
1785 |       // Only Token.FOR can have an Token.EMPTY other control structure
1786 |       // need something for the condition. Others need to be replaced
1787 |       // or the structure removed.
1788 |       parent.replaceChild(node, new Node(Token.EMPTY));
1789 |     } else {
1790 |       throw new IllegalStateException("Invalid attempt to remove node: " +
1791 |           node.toString() + " of "+ parent.toString());
1792 |     }
1793 |   }

===== M0482 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.NodeUtil.isLatin(String)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/NodeUtil.java
Original range: 2420-2430
Documentation:
/** Test if all characters in the string are in the Basic Latin (aka ASCII)
   * character set - that they have UTF-16 values equal to or below 0x7f.
   * This check can find which identifiers with Unicode characters need to be
   * escaped in order to allow resulting files to be processed by non-Unicode
   * aware UNIX tools and editors.
   * *
   * See http://en.wikipedia.org/wiki/Latin_characters_in_Unicode
   * for more on Basic Latin.
   *
   * @param s The string to be checked for ASCII-goodness.
   *
   * @return True if all characters in the string are in Basic Latin set.
   */
Method code:
2420 |   static boolean isLatin(String s) {
2421 |     char LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN = 0x7f;
2422 |     int len = s.length();
2423 |     for (int index = 0; index < len; index++) {
2424 |       char c = s.charAt(index);
2425 |       if (c > LARGEST_BASIC_LATIN) {
2426 |         return false;
2427 |       }
2428 |     }
2429 |     return true;
2430 |   }

Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.MakeDeclaredNamesUnique.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)

Method code:
141 |   @Override
142 |   public void visit(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
143 |     switch (n.getType()) {
144 |       case Token.NAME:
145 |         String newName = getReplacementName(n.getString());
146 |         if (newName != null) {
147 |           Renamer renamer = nameStack.peek();
148 |           if (renamer.stripConstIfReplaced()) {
149 |             // TODO(johnlenz): Do we need to do anything about the javadoc?
150 |             n.removeProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);

===== M0487 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.extractForInitializer(Node,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 496-543
Documentation:
/**
     * Bring the initializers out of FOR loops.  These need to be placed
     * before any associated LABEL nodes. This needs to be done from the top
     * level label first so this is called as a pre-order callback (from
     * shouldTraverse).
     *
     * @param n The node to inspect.
     * @param before The node to insert the initializer before.
     * @param beforeParent The parent of the node before which the initializer
     *     will be inserted.
     */
Method code:
496 |     private void extractForInitializer(
497 |         Node n, Node before, Node beforeParent) {
498 | 
499 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
500 |         next = c.getNext();
501 |         Node insertBefore = (before == null) ? c : before;
502 |         Node insertBeforeParent = (before == null) ? n : beforeParent;
503 |         switch (c.getType()) {
504 |           case Token.LABEL:
505 |             extractForInitializer(c, insertBefore, insertBeforeParent);
506 |             break;
507 |           case Token.FOR:
508 |             if (NodeUtil.isForIn(c)) {
509 |               Node first = c.getFirstChild();
510 |               if (first.getType() == Token.VAR) {
511 |                 // Transform:
512 |                 //    for (var a = 1 in b) {}
513 |                 // to:
514 |                 //    var a = 1; for (a in b) {};
515 |                 Node newStatement = first;
516 |                 // Clone just the node, to remove any initialization.
517 |                 Node name = newStatement.getFirstChild().cloneNode();
518 |                 first.getParent().replaceChild(first, name);
519 |                 insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
520 |                 reportCodeChange("FOR-IN var declaration");
521 |               }
522 |             } else if (c.getFirstChild().getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
523 |               Node init = c.getFirstChild();
524 |               Node empty = new Node(Token.EMPTY);
525 |               empty.copyInformationFrom(c);
526 |               c.replaceChild(init, empty);
527 | 
528 |               Node newStatement;
529 |               // Only VAR statements, and expressions are allowed,
530 |               // but are handled differently.
531 |               if (init.getType() == Token.VAR) {
532 |                 newStatement = init;
533 |               } else {
534 |                 newStatement = NodeUtil.newExpr(init);
535 |               }
536 | 
537 |               insertBeforeParent.addChildBefore(newStatement, insertBefore);
538 |               reportCodeChange("FOR initializer");
539 |             }
540 |             break;
541 |         }
542 |       }
543 |     }

85 |       String sourceStr) {

88 |       root = ParserRunner.parse(sourceName, sourceStr,

91 |           logger_);

===== M0502 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(String,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 114-142
Documentation:
/**
   * @param fnName The name of this function. This either the name of the
   *  variable to which the function is assigned or the name from the FUNCTION
   *  node.
   * @param fnNode The FUNCTION node of the function to inspect.
   * @return Whether the function node meets the minimum requirements for
   * inlining.
   */
Method code:
114 |   boolean doesFunctionMeetMinimumRequirements(
115 |       final String fnName, Node fnNode) {
116 |     Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
117 | 
118 |     // Basic restrictions on functions that can be inlined:
119 |     // 1) It contains a reference to itself.
120 |     // 2) It uses its parameters indirectly using "arguments" (it isn't
121 |     //    handled yet.
122 |     // 3) It references "eval". Inline a function containing eval can have
123 |     //    large performance implications.
124 | 
125 |     final String fnRecursionName = fnNode.getFirstChild().getString();
126 |     Preconditions.checkState(fnRecursionName != null);
127 | 
128 |     Predicate<Node> p = new Predicate<Node>(){
129 |       public boolean apply(Node n) {
130 |         if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
131 |           return n.getString().equals("arguments")
132 |             || n.getString().equals("eval")
133 |             || n.getString().equals(fnName)
134 |             || (!fnRecursionName.isEmpty()
135 |                 && n.getString().equals(fnRecursionName));
136 |         }
137 |         return false;
138 |       }
139 |     };
140 | 
141 |     return !NodeUtil.has(block, p, Predicates.<Node>alwaysTrue());
142 |   }

===== M0504 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.FunctionInjector.callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(NodeTraversal,Node,Node,Set<String>)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/FunctionInjector.java
Original range: 556-603
Documentation:
/**
   * Determines whether a function can be inlined at a particular call site.
   * - Don't inline if the calling function contains an inner function and
   * inlining would introduce new globals.
   */
Method code:
556 |   private boolean callMeetsBlockInliningRequirements(
557 |       NodeTraversal t, Node callNode, Node fnNode, Set<String> namesToAlias) {
558 |     // Note: functions that contain function definitions are filtered out
559 |     // in isCanidateFunction.
560 | 
561 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Determining if the called function contains VARs
562 |     // or if the caller contains inner functions accounts for 20% of the
563 |     // runtime cost of this pass.
564 | 
565 |     // Don't inline functions with var declarations into a scope with inner
566 |     // functions as the new vars would leak into the inner function and
567 |     // cause memory leaks.
568 |     boolean fnContainsVars = NodeUtil.has(
569 |         NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode),
570 |         new NodeUtil.MatchDeclaration(),
571 |         new NodeUtil.MatchShallowStatement());
572 |     boolean callerContainsFunction = false;
573 |     if (!t.inGlobalScope()) {
574 |       Node fnCaller = t.getScopeRoot();
575 |       Node fnCallerBody = fnCaller.getLastChild();
576 | 
577 |       callerContainsFunction = NodeUtil.containsFunction(fnCallerBody);
578 |     }
579 | 
580 |     if (fnContainsVars && callerContainsFunction) {
581 |       return false;
582 |     }
583 | 
584 |     // If the caller contains functions, verify we aren't adding any
585 |     // additional VAR declarations because aliasing is needed.
586 |     if (callerContainsFunction) {
587 |       Map<String, Node> args =
588 |           FunctionArgumentInjector.getFunctionCallParameterMap(
589 |               fnNode, callNode, this.safeNameIdSupplier);
590 |       boolean hasArgs = !args.isEmpty();
591 |       if (hasArgs) {
592 |         // Limit the inlining
593 |         Set<String> allNamesToAlias = Sets.newHashSet(namesToAlias);
594 |         FunctionArgumentInjector.maybeAddTempsForCallArguments(
595 |             fnNode, args, allNamesToAlias, compiler.getCodingConvention());
596 |         if (!allNamesToAlias.isEmpty()) {
597 |           return false;
598 |         }
599 |       }
600 |     }
601 | 
602 |     return true;
603 |   }

===== M0509 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.isEquivalentTo(Node,boolean,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1596-1655
Documentation:
/**
   * @param compareJsType Whether to compare the JSTypes of the nodes.
   * @param recurse Whether to compare the children of the current node, if
   *    not only the the count of the children are compared.
   * @return Whether this node is equivalent semantically to the provided node.
   */
Method code:
1596 |   boolean isEquivalentTo(Node node, boolean compareJsType, boolean recurse) {
1597 |     if (type != node.getType()
1598 |         || getChildCount() != node.getChildCount()
1599 |         || getNodeClass(this) != getNodeClass(node)) {
1600 |       return false;
1601 |     }
1602 | 
1603 |     if (compareJsType && !JSType.isEquivalent(jsType, node.getJSType())) {
1604 |       return false;
1605 |     }
1606 | 
1607 |     if (type == Token.ARRAYLIT) {
1608 |       try {
1609 |         int[] indices1 = (int[]) getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1610 |         int[] indices2 = (int[]) node.getProp(Node.SKIP_INDEXES_PROP);
1611 |         if (indices1 == null) {
1612 |           if (indices2 != null) {
1613 |             return false;
1614 |           }
1615 |         } else if (indices2 == null) {
1616 |           return false;
1617 |         } else if (indices1.length != indices2.length) {
1618 |           return false;
1619 |         } else {
1620 |           for (int i = 0; i < indices1.length; i++) {
1621 |             if (indices1[i] != indices2[i]) {
1622 |               return false;
1623 |             }
1624 |           }
1625 |         }
1626 |       } catch (Exception e) {
1627 |         return false;
1628 |       }
1629 |     } else if (type == Token.INC || type == Token.DEC) {
1630 |       int post1 = this.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1631 |       int post2 = node.getIntProp(INCRDECR_PROP);
1632 |       if (post1 != post2) {
1633 |         return false;
1634 |       }
1635 |     } else if (type == Token.STRING) {
1636 |       int quoted1 = this.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1637 |       int quoted2 = node.getIntProp(QUOTED_PROP);
1638 |       if (quoted1 != quoted2) {
1639 |         return false;
1640 |       }
1641 |     }
1642 | 
1643 |     if (recurse) {
1644 |       Node n, n2;
1645 |       for (n = first, n2 = node.first;
1646 |            n != null;
1647 |            n = n.next, n2 = n2.next) {
1648 |         if (!n.isEquivalentTo(n2, compareJsType, true)) {
1649 |           return false;
1650 |         }
1651 |       }
1652 |     }
1653 | 
1654 |     return true;
1655 |   }

692 |   public void replaceChild(Node child, Node newChild) {

Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.propToString(int)

// x..y or x..@i
Method code:
172 |   private static final String propToString(int propType) {
173 |       switch (propType) {
174 |         case LOCAL_BLOCK_PROP:   return "local_block";
175 |         case OBJECT_IDS_PROP:    return "object_ids_prop";
176 |         case CATCH_SCOPE_PROP:   return "catch_scope_prop";

===== M0535 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.ParserRunner.parse(String,String,Config,ErrorReporter,Logger)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/ParserRunner.java
Original range: 90-132
Documentation:
/**
   * Parses the JavaScript text given by a reader.
   *
   * @param sourceName The filename.
   * @param sourceString Source code from the file.
   * @param errorReporter An error.
   * @param logger A logger.
   * @return The AST of the given text.
   * @throws IOException
   */
Method code:
90 |   public static Node parse(String sourceName,
91 |                            String sourceString,
92 |                            Config config,
93 |                            ErrorReporter errorReporter,
94 |                            Logger logger) throws IOException {
95 |     Context cx = Context.enter();
96 |     cx.setErrorReporter(errorReporter);
97 |     cx.setLanguageVersion(Context.VERSION_1_5);
98 |     CompilerEnvirons compilerEnv = new CompilerEnvirons();
99 |     compilerEnv.initFromContext(cx);
100 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingComments(true);
101 |     compilerEnv.setRecordingLocalJsDocComments(true);
102 |     // ES5 specifically allows trailing commas
103 |     compilerEnv.setWarnTrailingComma(
104 |         config.languageMode == LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3);
105 | 
106 |     if (config.isIdeMode || config.languageMode != LanguageMode.ECMASCRIPT3) {
107 |       // Do our own identifier check for ECMASCRIPT 5
108 |       compilerEnv.setReservedKeywordAsIdentifier(true);
109 |       compilerEnv.setAllowKeywordAsObjectPropertyName(true);
110 |     }
111 | 
112 |     if (config.isIdeMode) {
113 |       compilerEnv.setAllowMemberExprAsFunctionName(true);
114 |     }
115 | 
116 |     Parser p = new Parser(compilerEnv, errorReporter);
117 |     AstRoot astRoot = null;
118 |     try {
119 |       astRoot = p.parse(sourceString, sourceName, 1);
120 |     } catch (EvaluatorException e) {
121 |       logger.info("Error parsing " + sourceName + ": " + e.getMessage());
122 |     } finally {
123 |       Context.exit();
124 |     }
125 |     Node root = null;
126 |     if (astRoot != null) {
127 |       root = IRFactory.transformTree(
128 |           astRoot, sourceString, config, errorReporter);
129 |       root.setIsSyntheticBlock(true);
130 |     }
131 |     return root;
132 |   }

===== M0540 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.TransformDispatcher.processFunctionNode(FunctionNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 582-639
Documentation:

Method code:
582 |     @Override
583 |     Node processFunctionNode(FunctionNode functionNode) {
584 |       Name name = functionNode.getFunctionName();
585 |       Boolean isUnnamedFunction = false;
586 |       if (name == null) {
587 |         int functionType = functionNode.getFunctionType();
588 |         if (functionType != FunctionNode.FUNCTION_EXPRESSION) {
589 |           errorReporter.error(
590 |             "unnamed function statement",
591 |             sourceName,
592 |             functionNode.getLineno(), "", 0);
593 |         }
594 |         name = new Name();
595 |         name.setIdentifier("");
596 |         isUnnamedFunction = true;
597 |       }
598 |       Node node = newNode(Token.FUNCTION);
599 |       Node newName = transform(name);
600 |       if (isUnnamedFunction) {
601 |         // Old Rhino tagged the empty name node with the line number of the
602 |         // declaration.
603 |         newName.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
604 |         // TODO(bowdidge) Mark line number of paren correctly.
605 |         // Same problem as below - the left paren might not be on the
606 |         // same line as the function keyword.
607 |         int lpColumn = functionNode.getAbsolutePosition() +
608 |             functionNode.getLp();
609 |         newName.setCharno(position2charno(lpColumn));
610 |       }
611 | 
612 |       node.addChildToBack(newName);
613 |       Node lp = newNode(Token.LP);
614 |       // The left paren's complicated because it's not represented by an
615 |       // AstNode, so there's nothing that has the actual line number that it
616 |       // appeared on.  We know the paren has to appear on the same line as the
617 |       // function name (or else a semicolon will be inserted.)  If there's no
618 |       // function name, assume the paren was on the same line as the function.
619 |       // TODO(bowdidge): Mark line number of paren correctly.
620 |       Name fnName = functionNode.getFunctionName();
621 |       if (fnName != null) {
622 |         lp.setLineno(fnName.getLineno());
623 |       } else {
624 |         lp.setLineno(functionNode.getLineno());
625 |       }
626 |       int lparenCharno = functionNode.getLp() +
627 |           functionNode.getAbsolutePosition();
628 | 
629 |       lp.setCharno(position2charno(lparenCharno));
630 |       for (AstNode param : functionNode.getParams()) {
631 |         lp.addChildToBack(transform(param));
632 |       }
633 |       node.addChildToBack(lp);
634 | 
635 |       Node bodyNode = transform(functionNode.getBody());
636 |       parseDirectives(bodyNode);
637 |       node.addChildToBack(bodyNode);
638 |      return node;
639 |     }

===== M0543 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.addNonEmptyStatement(Node,Context,boolean)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 773-829
Documentation:
/**
   * Adds a block or expression, substituting a VOID with an empty statement.
   * This is used for "for (...);" and "if (...);" type statements.
   *
   * @param n The node to print.
   * @param context The context to determine how the node should be printed.
   */
Method code:
773 |   private void addNonEmptyStatement(
774 |       Node n, Context context, boolean allowNonBlockChild) {
775 |     Node nodeToProcess = n;
776 | 
777 |     if (!allowNonBlockChild && n.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
778 |       throw new Error("Missing BLOCK child.");
779 |     }
780 | 
781 |     // Strip unneeded blocks, that is blocks with <2 children unless
782 |     // the CodePrinter specifically wants to keep them.
783 |     if (n.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
784 |       int count = getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2);
785 |       if (count == 0) {
786 |         if (cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks()) {
787 |           cc.beginBlock();
788 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
789 |         } else {
790 |           cc.endStatement(true);
791 |         }
792 |         return;
793 |       }
794 | 
795 |       if (count == 1) {
796 |         // Hack around a couple of browser bugs:
797 |         //   Safari needs a block around function declarations.
798 |         //   IE6/7 needs a block around DOs.
799 |         Node firstAndOnlyChild = getFirstNonEmptyChild(n);
800 |         boolean alwaysWrapInBlock = cc.shouldPreserveExtraBlocks();
801 |         if (alwaysWrapInBlock || isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(firstAndOnlyChild)) {
802 |           cc.beginBlock();
803 |           add(firstAndOnlyChild, Context.STATEMENT);
804 |           cc.maybeLineBreak();
805 |           cc.endBlock(cc.breakAfterBlockFor(n, context == Context.STATEMENT));
806 |           return;
807 |         } else {
808 |           // Continue with the only child.
809 |           nodeToProcess = firstAndOnlyChild;
810 |         }
811 |       }
812 | 
813 |       if (count > 1) {
814 |         context = Context.PRESERVE_BLOCK;
815 |       }
816 |     }
817 | 
818 |     if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.EMPTY) {
819 |       cc.endStatement(true);
820 |     } else {
821 |       add(nodeToProcess, context);
822 | 
823 |       // VAR doesn't include ';' since it gets used in expressions - so any
824 |       // VAR in a statement context needs a call to endStatement() here.
825 |       if (nodeToProcess.getType() == Token.VAR) {
826 |         cc.endStatement();
827 |       }
828 |     }
829 |   }

===== M0552 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.parsing.IRFactory.setSourceInfo(Node,AstNode)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/parsing/IRFactory.java
Original range: 296-313
Documentation:

Method code:
296 |   private void setSourceInfo(Node irNode, AstNode node) {
297 |     // If we have a named function, set the position to that of the name.
298 |     if (irNode.getType() == Token.FUNCTION &&
299 |         irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno() != -1) {
300 |       irNode.setLineno(irNode.getFirstChild().getLineno());
301 |       irNode.setCharno(irNode.getFirstChild().getCharno());
302 |     } else {
303 |       if (irNode.getLineno() == -1) {
304 |         // If we didn't already set the line, then set it now.  This avoids
305 |         // cases like ParenthesizedExpression where we just return a previous
306 |         // node, but don't want the new node to get its parent's line number.
307 |         int lineno = node.getLineno();
308 |         irNode.setLineno(lineno);
309 |         int charno = position2charno(node.getAbsolutePosition());
310 |         irNode.setCharno(charno);
311 |       }
312 |     }
313 |   }

120 |     new NodeTraversal(
121 |         compiler, new NormalizeStatements(compiler, assertOnChange))
122 |         .traverseRoots(externs, root);
123 |     if (MAKE_LOCAL_NAMES_UNIQUE) {
124 |       MakeDeclaredNamesUnique renamer = new MakeDeclaredNamesUnique();
125 |       NodeTraversal t = new NodeTraversal(compiler, renamer);
126 |       t.traverseRoots(externs, root);
127 |     }
128 |     // It is important that removeDuplicateDeclarations runs after
129 |     // MakeDeclaredNamesUnique in order for catch block exception names to be
130 |     // handled properly. Specifically, catch block exception names are
131 |     // only valid within the catch block, but our currect Scope logic
132 |     // has no concept of this and includes it in the containing function
133 |     // (or global scope). MakeDeclaredNamesUnique makes the catch exception
134 |     // names unique so that removeDuplicateDeclarations() will properly handle
135 |     // cases where a function scope variable conflict with a exception name:
136 |     //   function f() {
137 |     //      try {throw 0;} catch(e) {e; /* catch scope 'e'*/}
138 |     //      var e = 1; // f scope 'e'
139 |     //   }
140 |     // otherwise 'var e = 1' would be rewritten as 'e = 1'.
141 |     // TODO(johnlenz): Introduce a seperate scope for catch nodes.
142 |     removeDuplicateDeclarations(externs, root);
143 |     new PropagateConstantAnnotationsOverVars(compiler, assertOnChange)
144 |         .process(externs, root);
145 | 
146 |     if (!compiler.getLifeCycleStage().isNormalized()) {
147 |       compiler.setLifeCycleStage(LifeCycleStage.NORMALIZED);
148 |     }
149 |   }

===== M0557 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.getNonEmptyChildCount(Node,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 1090-1101
Documentation:
/**
   * @param maxCount The maximum number of children to look for.
   * @return The number of children of this node that are non empty up to
   * maxCount.
   */
Method code:
1090 |   private static int getNonEmptyChildCount(Node n, int maxCount) {
1091 |     int i = 0;
1092 |     Node c = n.getFirstChild();
1093 |     for (; c != null && i < maxCount; c = c.getNext()) {
1094 |       if (c.getType() == Token.BLOCK) {
1095 |         i += getNonEmptyChildCount(c, maxCount-i);
1096 |       } else if (c.getType() != Token.EMPTY) {
1097 |         i++;
1098 |       }
1099 |     }
1100 |     return i;
1101 |   }

===== M0573 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.JSType.isSubtype(JSType,JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/JSType.java
Original range: 888-915
Documentation:
/**
   * A generic implementation meant to be used as a helper for common subtyping
   * cases.
   */
Method code:
888 |   static boolean isSubtype(JSType thisType, JSType thatType) {
889 |     // unknown
890 |     if (thatType.isUnknownType()) {
891 |       return true;
892 |     }
893 |     // equality
894 |     if (thisType.isEquivalentTo(thatType)) {
895 |       return true;
896 |     }
897 |     // all type
898 |     if (thatType.isAllType()) {
899 |       return true;
900 |     }
901 |     // unions
902 |     if (thatType instanceof UnionType) {
903 |       UnionType union = (UnionType)thatType;
904 |       for (JSType element : union.alternates) {
905 |         if (thisType.isSubtype(element)) {
906 |           return true;
907 |         }
908 |       }
909 |     }
910 |     // named types
911 |     if (thatType instanceof NamedType) {
912 |       return thisType.isSubtype(((NamedType)thatType).getReferencedType());
913 |     }
914 |     return false;
915 |   }

===== M0584 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.CodeGenerator.isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/CodeGenerator.java
Original range: 835-855
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the Node is a DO or FUNCTION (with or without
   * labels).
   */
Method code:
835 |   private boolean isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(Node n) {
836 |     if (n.getType() == Token.LABEL) {
837 |       Node labeledStatement = n.getLastChild();
838 |       if (labeledStatement.getType() != Token.BLOCK) {
839 |         return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(labeledStatement);
840 |       } else {
841 |         // For labels with block children, we need to ensure that a
842 |         // labeled FUNCTION or DO isn't generated when extraneous BLOCKs
843 |         // are skipped.
844 |         if (getNonEmptyChildCount(n, 2) == 1) {
845 |           return isOneExactlyFunctionOrDo(getFirstNonEmptyChild(n));
846 |         } else {
847 |           // Either a empty statement or an block with more than one child,
848 |           // way it isn't a FUNCTION or DO.
849 |           return false;
850 |         }
851 |       }
852 |     } else {
853 |       return (n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION || n.getType() == Token.DO);
854 |     }
855 |   }

Method code:
1888 |   public Node cloneTree() {
1889 |     Node result = cloneNode();
1890 |     for (Node n2 = getFirstChild(); n2 != null; n2 = n2.getNext()) {

118 |   public T process(AstNode node) {

136 |       case Token.MUL:
137 |       case Token.NE:
138 |       case Token.OR:

151 |       case Token.ASSIGN_BITXOR:
152 |       case Token.ASSIGN_DIV:

165 |       case Token.NOT:

167 |       case Token.TYPEOF:
168 |       case Token.VOID:
169 |         return processUnaryExpression((UnaryExpression) node);
170 |       case Token.BLOCK:
171 |         if (node instanceof Block) {
172 |           return processBlock((Block) node);
173 |         } else  if (node instanceof Scope) {
174 |           return processScope((Scope) node);
175 |         } else {
176 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
177 |                                           node.getClass() +
178 |                                           " type: " +
179 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
180 |         }
181 |       case Token.BREAK:
182 |         return processBreakStatement((BreakStatement) node);
183 |       case Token.CALL:
184 |         return processFunctionCall((FunctionCall) node);
185 |       case Token.CASE:

187 |         return processSwitchCase((SwitchCase) node);
188 |       case Token.CATCH:
189 |       case Token.FINALLY:
190 |         return processCatchClause((CatchClause) node);
191 |       case Token.COLON:
192 |         return processObjectProperty((ObjectProperty) node);
193 |       case Token.CONTINUE:
194 |         return processContinueStatement((ContinueStatement) node);
195 |       case Token.DO:
196 |         return processDoLoop((DoLoop) node);
197 |       case Token.EMPTY:
198 |         return processEmptyExpression((EmptyExpression) node);
199 |       case Token.EXPR_RESULT:
200 |       case Token.EXPR_VOID:
201 |         if (node instanceof ExpressionStatement) {
202 |           return processExpressionStatement((ExpressionStatement) node);
203 |         } else  if (node instanceof LabeledStatement) {
204 |           return processLabeledStatement((LabeledStatement) node);
205 |         } else {

207 |                                           node.getClass() +
208 |                                           " type: " +
209 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
210 |         }

215 |       case Token.TRUE:

217 |       case Token.FOR:
218 |         if (node instanceof ForInLoop) {
219 |           return processForInLoop((ForInLoop) node);
220 |         } else  if (node instanceof ForLoop) {
221 |           return processForLoop((ForLoop) node);
222 |         } else {
