224 |                                           node.getClass() +
225 |                                           " type: " +
226 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
227 |         }
228 |       case Token.FUNCTION:

230 |       case Token.GETELEM:
231 |         return processElementGet((ElementGet) node);
232 |       case Token.GETPROP:
233 |         return processPropertyGet((PropertyGet) node);
234 |       case Token.HOOK:

236 |       case Token.IF:

238 |       case Token.LABEL:

244 |       case Token.NEW:

246 |       case Token.NUMBER:

248 |       case Token.OBJECTLIT:
249 |         return processObjectLiteral((ObjectLiteral) node);
250 |       case Token.REGEXP:
251 |         return processRegExpLiteral((RegExpLiteral) node);
252 |       case Token.RETURN:
253 |         return processReturnStatement((ReturnStatement) node);
254 |       case Token.SCRIPT:
255 |         return processAstRoot((AstRoot) node);
256 |       case Token.STRING:
257 |         return processStringLiteral((StringLiteral) node);
258 |       case Token.SWITCH:
259 |         return processSwitchStatement((SwitchStatement) node);
260 |       case Token.THROW:
261 |         return processThrowStatement((ThrowStatement) node);
262 |       case Token.TRY:
263 |         return processTryStatement((TryStatement) node);
264 |       case Token.CONST:
265 |       case Token.VAR:
266 |         if (node instanceof VariableDeclaration) {
267 |           return processVariableDeclaration((VariableDeclaration) node);
268 |         } else  if (node instanceof VariableInitializer) {
269 |           return processVariableInitializer((VariableInitializer) node);
270 |         } else {
271 |           throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected node type.  class: " +
272 |                                           node.getClass() +
273 |                                           " type: " +
274 |                                           Token.typeToName(node.getType()));
275 |         }
276 |       case Token.WHILE:
277 |         return processWhileLoop((WhileLoop) node);
278 |       case Token.WITH:
279 |         return processWithStatement((WithStatement) node);
280 |     }
281 |     return processIllegalToken(node);
282 |   }

===== M0594 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidateFunctions.findNamedFunctions(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 167-196
Documentation:

Method code:
167 |     public void findNamedFunctions(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
168 |       if (!NodeUtil.isStatement(n)) {
169 |         // There aren't any interesting functions here.
170 |         return;
171 |       }
172 | 
173 |       switch (n.getType()) {
174 |         // Functions expressions in the form of:
175 |         //   var fooFn = function(x) { return ... }
176 |         case Token.VAR:
177 |           Preconditions.checkState(n.hasOneChild());
178 |           Node nameNode = n.getFirstChild();
179 |           if (nameNode.getType() == Token.NAME && nameNode.hasChildren()
180 |               && nameNode.getFirstChild().getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
181 |             maybeAddFunction(new FunctionVar(n), t.getModule());
182 |           }
183 |           break;
184 | 
185 |         // Named functions
186 |         // function Foo(x) { return ... }
187 |         case Token.FUNCTION:
188 |           Preconditions.checkState(NodeUtil.isStatementBlock(parent)
189 |               || parent.getType() == Token.LABEL);
190 |           if (!NodeUtil.isFunctionExpression(n)) {
191 |             Function fn = new NamedFunction(n);
192 |             maybeAddFunction(fn, t.getModule());
193 |           }
194 |           break;
195 |       }
196 |     }

/**
   * Checks if the given function matches the criteria for an inlinable
   * function, and if so, adds it to our set of inlinable functions.
   */
Method code:
490 |   boolean isDirectCallNodeReplacementPossible(Node fnNode) {
491 |     // Only inline single-statement functions
492 |     Node block = NodeUtil.getFunctionBody(fnNode);
493 | 
494 |     // Check if this function is suitable for direct replacement of a CALL node:
495 |     // a function that consists of single return that returns an expression.

502 |           && block.getFirstChild().getFirstChild() != null) {
503 |         return true;
504 |       }
505 |     }
506 | 
507 |     return false;
508 |   }

===== M0614 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.Node.mergeLineCharNo(int,int)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/Node.java
Original range: 1159-1167
Documentation:
/**
   * Merges the line number and character number in one integer. The Character
   * number takes the first 12 bits and the line number takes the rest. If
   * the character number is greater than <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code> it is
   * adjusted to <code>2<sup>12</sup>-1</code>.
   */
Method code:
1159 |   protected static int mergeLineCharNo(int lineno, int charno) {
1160 |     if (lineno < 0 || charno < 0) {
1161 |       return -1;
1162 |     } else if ((charno & ~COLUMN_MASK) != 0) {
1163 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | COLUMN_MASK;
1164 |     } else {
1165 |       return lineno << COLUMN_BITS | (charno & COLUMN_MASK);
1166 |     }
1167 |   }

Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Compiler.java
Original range: 221-277
Documentation:
/**
   * Initialize the compiler options. Only necessary if you're not doing
   * a normal compile() job.
   */
Method code:
221 |   public void initOptions(CompilerOptions options) {
222 |     this.options = options;
223 |     this.options.initDeserializedProperties();
224 |     if (errorManager == null) {
225 |       if (outStream == null) {
226 |         setErrorManager(
227 |             new LoggerErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), logger));
228 |       } else {
229 |         PrintStreamErrorManager printer =
230 |             new PrintStreamErrorManager(createMessageFormatter(), outStream);
231 |         printer.setSummaryDetailLevel(options.summaryDetailLevel);
232 |         setErrorManager(printer);
233 |       }
234 |     }
235 | 
236 |     // DiagnosticGroups override the plain checkTypes option.
237 |     if (options.enables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
238 |       options.checkTypes = true;
239 |     } else if (options.disables(DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_TYPES)) {
240 |       options.checkTypes = false;
241 |     } else if (!options.checkTypes) {
242 |       // If DiagnosticGroups did not override the plain checkTypes
243 |       // option, and checkTypes is enabled, then turn off the
244 |       // parser type warnings.
245 |       options.setWarningLevel(
246 |           DiagnosticGroup.forType(
247 |               RhinoErrorReporter.TYPE_PARSE_ERROR),
248 |           CheckLevel.OFF);
249 |     }
250 | 
251 |     if (options.checkGlobalThisLevel.isOn()) {
252 |       options.setWarningLevel(
253 |           DiagnosticGroups.GLOBAL_THIS,
254 |           options.checkGlobalThisLevel);
255 |     }
256 | 
257 |     // Initialize the warnings guard.
258 |     List<WarningsGuard> guards = Lists.newArrayList();
259 |     guards.add(
260 |         new SuppressDocWarningsGuard(
261 |             getDiagnosticGroups().getRegisteredGroups()));
262 |     guards.add(options.getWarningsGuard());
263 | 
264 |     // All passes must run the variable check. This synthesizes
265 |     // variables later so that the compiler doesn't crash. It also
266 |     // checks the externs file for validity. If you don't want to warn
267 |     // about missing variable declarations, we shut that specific
268 |     // error off.
269 |     if (!options.checkSymbols &&
270 |         (warningsGuard == null || !warningsGuard.disables(
271 |             DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES))) {
272 |       guards.add(new DiagnosticGroupWarningsGuard(
273 |           DiagnosticGroups.CHECK_VARIABLES, CheckLevel.OFF));
274 |     }
275 | 
276 |     this.warningsGuard = new ComposeWarningsGuard(guards);
277 |   }

Method code:
350 |     private void annotateConstantsByConvention(Node n, Node parent) {
351 |       Preconditions.checkState(
352 |           n.getType() == Token.NAME
353 |           || n.getType() == Token.STRING
354 |           || n.getType() == Token.GET
355 |           || n.getType() == Token.SET);
356 | 
357 |       // There are only two cases where a string token
358 |       // may be a variable reference: The right side of a GETPROP
359 |       // or an OBJECTLIT key.
360 |       boolean isObjLitKey = NodeUtil.isObjectLitKey(n, parent);
361 |       boolean isProperty = isObjLitKey ||
362 |           (parent.getType() == Token.GETPROP &&
363 |            parent.getLastChild() == n);
364 |       if (n.getType() == Token.NAME || isProperty) {
365 |         boolean isMarkedConstant = n.getBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME);
366 |         if (!isMarkedConstant &&
367 |             NodeUtil.isConstantByConvention(
368 |                 compiler.getCodingConvention(), n, parent)) {
369 |           if (assertOnChange) {
370 |             String name = n.getString();
371 |             throw new IllegalStateException(
372 |                 "Unexpected const change.\n" +
373 |                 "  name: "+ name + "\n" +
374 |                 "  parent:" + n.getParent().toStringTree());
375 |           }
376 |           n.putBooleanProp(Node.IS_CONSTANT_NAME, true);
377 |         }
378 |       }
379 |     }

===== M0628 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.normalizeLabels(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 464-483
Documentation:
/**
     * Limit the number of special cases where LABELs need to be handled. Only
     * BLOCK and loops are allowed to be labeled.  Loop labels must remain in
     * place as the named continues are not allowed for labeled blocks.
     */
Method code:
464 |     private void normalizeLabels(Node n) {
465 |       Preconditions.checkArgument(n.getType() == Token.LABEL);
466 | 
467 |       Node last = n.getLastChild();
468 |       switch (last.getType()) {
469 |         case Token.LABEL:
470 |         case Token.BLOCK:
471 |         case Token.FOR:
472 |         case Token.WHILE:
473 |         case Token.DO:
474 |           return;
475 |         default:
476 |           Node block = new Node(Token.BLOCK);
477 |           block.copyInformationFrom(last);
478 |           n.replaceChild(last, block);
479 |           block.addChildToFront(last);
480 |           reportCodeChange("LABEL normalization");
481 |           return;
482 |       }
483 |     }

2647 |   static boolean has(Node node,

===== M0644 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.Normalize.NormalizeStatements.splitVarDeclarations(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/Normalize.java
Original range: 553-571
Documentation:
/**
     * Split a var node such as:
     *   var a, b;
     * into individual statements:
     *   var a;
     *   var b;
     * @param n The whose children we should inspect.
     */
Method code:
553 |     private void splitVarDeclarations(Node n) {
554 |       for (Node next, c = n.getFirstChild(); c != null; c = next) {
555 |         next = c.getNext();
556 |         if (c.getType() == Token.VAR) {
557 |           if (assertOnChange && !c.hasChildren()) {
558 |             throw new IllegalStateException("Empty VAR node.");
559 |           }
560 | 
561 |           while (c.getFirstChild() != c.getLastChild()) {
562 |             Node name = c.getFirstChild();
563 |             c.removeChild(name);
564 |             Node newVar = new Node(
565 |                 Token.VAR, name, n.getLineno(), n.getCharno());
566 |             n.addChildBefore(newVar, c);
567 |             reportCodeChange("VAR with multiple children");
568 |           }
569 |         }
570 |       }
571 |     }

/**
   * Check for uses of the named value that imply a pass-by-value
   * parameter is expected.  This is used to prevent cases like:
   *
   *   function (x) {
   *     x=2;
   *     return x;
   *   }
   *
   * We don't want "undefined" to be substituted for "x", and get
   *   undefined=2
   *
   * @param n The node in question.
   * @param parent The parent of the node.
   * @param names The set of names to check.
   */
Method code:
163 |   private static Set<String> findModifiedParameters(
164 |       Node n, Node parent, Set<String> names, Set<String> unsafe) {
165 |     Preconditions.checkArgument(unsafe != null);
166 |     if (n.getType() == Token.NAME) {
167 |       if (names.contains(n.getString())) {
168 |         if (canNameValueChange(n, parent)) {
169 |           unsafe.add(n.getString());
170 |         }
171 |       }
172 |     }
173 | 
174 |     for (Node c : n.children()) {
175 |       findModifiedParameters(c, n, names, unsafe);
176 |     }
177 | 
178 |     return unsafe;
179 |   }

Original range: 441-456
Documentation:
/**
     * Parse the directives, encode them in the AST, and remove their nodes.
     *
     * For information on ES5 directives, see section 14.1 of
     * Ecma-262, Edition 5.
     *
     * It would be nice if Rhino would eventually take care of this for
     * us, but right now their directive-processing is a one-off.
     */
Method code:
441 |     private void parseDirectives(Node node) {
442 |       // Remove all the directives, and encode them in the AST.
443 |       Set<String> directives = null;
444 |       while (isDirective(node.getFirstChild())) {
445 |         String directive = node.removeFirstChild().getFirstChild().getString();
446 |         if (directives == null) {
447 |           directives = Sets.newHashSet(directive);
448 |         } else {
449 |           directives.add(directive);
450 |         }
451 |       }
452 | 
453 |       if (directives != null) {
454 |         node.setDirectives(directives);
455 |       }
456 |     }

   *     {@code true}.
   * @return a tree hierarchy representing a typed argument list
   */
Method code:
1158 |   private Node createParameters(boolean lastVarArgs, JSType... parameterTypes) {
1159 |     FunctionParamBuilder builder = new FunctionParamBuilder(this);
1160 |     int max = parameterTypes.length - 1;
1161 |     for (int i = 0; i <= max; i++) {
1162 |       if (lastVarArgs && i == max) {
1163 |         builder.addVarArgs(parameterTypes[i]);
1164 |       } else {
1165 |         builder.addRequiredParams(parameterTypes[i]);
1166 |       }
1167 |     }
1168 |     return builder.build();
1169 |   }

===== M0675 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.getSimplifiedName(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 137-149
Documentation:
/**
   * Extract a name from a node.  In the case of GETPROP nodes,
   * replace the namespace or object expression with "this" for
   * simplicity and correctness at the expense of inefficiencies due
   * to higher chances of name collisions.
   *
   * TODO(user) revisit.  it would be helpful to at least use fully
   * qualified names in the case of namespaces.  Might not matter as
   * much if this pass runs after "collapsing properties".
   */
Method code:
137 |   private static String getSimplifiedName(Node node) {
138 |     if (NodeUtil.isName(node)) {
139 |       String name = node.getString();
140 |       if (name != null && !name.isEmpty()) {
141 |         return name;
142 |       } else {
143 |         return null;
144 |       }
145 |     } else if (NodeUtil.isGetProp(node)) {
146 |       return "this." + node.getLastChild().getString();
147 |     }
148 |     return null;
149 |   }

===== M0677 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.SimpleDefinitionFinder.DefinitionGatheringCallback.visit(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/SimpleDefinitionFinder.java
Original range: 158-262
Documentation:

Method code:
158 |     @Override
159 |     public void visit(NodeTraversal traversal, Node node, Node parent) {
160 |       // Arguments of external functions should not count as name
161 |       // definitions.  They are placeholder names for documentation
162 |       // purposes only which are not reachable from anywhere.
163 |       if (inExterns && NodeUtil.isName(node) && parent.getType() == Token.LP) {
164 |         return;
165 |       }
166 | 
167 |       Definition def =
168 |           DefinitionsRemover.getDefinition(node, inExterns);
169 |       if (def != null) {
170 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(def.getLValue());
171 |         if (name != null) {
172 |           Node rValue = def.getRValue();
173 |           if ((rValue != null) &&
174 |               !NodeUtil.isImmutableValue(rValue) &&
175 |               !NodeUtil.isFunction(rValue)) {
176 | 
177 |             // Unhandled complex expression
178 |             Definition unknownDef =
179 |                 new UnknownDefinition(def.getLValue(), inExterns);
180 |             def = unknownDef;
181 |           }
182 | 
183 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this stub dropping code if it becomes
184 |           // illegal to have untyped stubs in the externs definitions.
185 |           if (inExterns) {
186 |             // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
187 |             //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
188 | 
189 |             List<Definition> stubsToRemove = Lists.newArrayList();
190 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
191 | 
192 |             // If there is no qualified name for this, then there will be
193 |             // no stubs to remove. This will happen if node is an object
194 |             // literal key.
195 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
196 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
197 |                 if (prevDef instanceof ExternalNameOnlyDefinition
198 |                     && !jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
199 |                   String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
200 |                   if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
201 |                     // Drop this stub, there is a real definition.
202 |                     stubsToRemove.add(prevDef);
203 |                   }
204 |                 }
205 |               }
206 | 
207 |               for (Definition prevDef : stubsToRemove) {
208 |                 nameDefinitionMultimap.remove(name, prevDef);
209 |               }
210 |             }
211 |           }
212 | 
213 |           nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, def);
214 |           definitionSiteMap.put(node,
215 |                                 new DefinitionSite(node,
216 |                                                    def,
217 |                                                    traversal.getModule(),
218 |                                                    traversal.inGlobalScope(),
219 |                                                    inExterns));
220 |         }
221 |       }
222 | 
223 |       if (inExterns && (parent != null) && NodeUtil.isExpressionNode(parent)) {
224 |         String name = getSimplifiedName(node);
225 |         if (name != null) {
226 | 
227 |           // TODO(johnlenz) : remove this code if it becomes illegal to have
228 |           // stubs in the externs definitions.
229 | 
230 |           // We need special handling of untyped externs stubs here:
231 |           //    the stub should be dropped if the name is provided elsewhere.
232 |           // We can't just drop the stub now as it needs to be used as the
233 |           //    externs definition if no other definition is provided.
234 | 
235 |           boolean dropStub = false;
236 |           if (!jsdocContainsDeclarations(node)) {
237 |             String qualifiedName = node.getQualifiedName();
238 |             if (qualifiedName != null) {
239 |               for (Definition prevDef : nameDefinitionMultimap.get(name)) {
240 |                 String prevName = prevDef.getLValue().getQualifiedName();
241 |                 if (qualifiedName.equals(prevName)) {
242 |                   dropStub = true;
243 |                   break;
244 |                 }
245 |               }
246 |             }
247 |           }
248 | 
249 |           if (!dropStub) {
250 |             // Incomplete definition
251 |             Definition definition = new ExternalNameOnlyDefinition(node);
252 |             nameDefinitionMultimap.put(name, definition);
253 |             definitionSiteMap.put(node,
254 |                                   new DefinitionSite(node,
255 |                                                      definition,
256 |                                                      traversal.getModule(),
257 |                                                      traversal.inGlobalScope(),
258 |                                                      inExterns));
259 |           }
260 |         }
261 |       }
262 |     }

===== M0680 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.isCandidateUsage(Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 418-452
Documentation:
/**
   * @return Whether the name is used in a way that might be a candidate
   *   for inlining.
   */
Method code:
418 |   static boolean isCandidateUsage(Node name) {
419 |     Node parent = name.getParent();
420 |     Preconditions.checkState(name.getType() == Token.NAME);
421 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.VAR || parent.getType() == Token.FUNCTION) {
422 |       // This is a declaration.  Duplicate declarations are handle during
423 |       // function candidate gathering.
424 |       return true;
425 |     }
426 | 
427 |     if (parent.getType() == Token.CALL && parent.getFirstChild() == name) {
428 |       // This is a normal reference to the function.
429 |       return true;
430 |     }
431 | 
432 |     // Check for a ".call" to the named function:
433 |     //   CALL
434 |     //     GETPROP/GETELEM
435 |     //       NAME
436 |     //       STRING == "call"
437 |     //     This-Value
438 |     //     Function-parameter-1
439 |     //     ...
440 |     if (NodeUtil.isGet(parent)
441 |          && name == parent.getFirstChild()
442 |          && name.getNext().getType() == Token.STRING
443 |          && name.getNext().getString().equals("call")) {
444 |       Node gramps = name.getAncestor(2);
445 |       if (gramps.getType() == Token.CALL
446 |           && gramps.getFirstChild() == parent) {
447 |         // Yep, a ".call".
448 |         return true;
449 |       }
450 |     }
451 |     return false;
452 |   }

===== M0681 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.jscomp.InlineFunctions.FindCandidatesReferences.checkNameUsage(NodeTraversal,Node,Node)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/jscomp/InlineFunctions.java
Original range: 539-578
Documentation:
/**
     * Find functions that can be inlined.
     */
Method code:
539 |     private void checkNameUsage(NodeTraversal t, Node n, Node parent) {
540 |       Preconditions.checkState(n.getType() == Token.NAME);
541 | 
542 |       if (isCandidateUsage(n)) {
543 |         return;
544 |       }
545 | 
546 |       // Other refs to a function name remove its candidacy for inlining
547 |       String name = n.getString();
548 |       FunctionState fs = fns.get(name);
549 |       if (fs == null) {
550 |         return;
551 |       }
552 | 
553 |       // Unlike normal call/new parameters, references passed to
554 |       // JSCompiler_ObjectPropertyString are not aliases of a value, but
555 |       // a reference to the name itself, as such the value of the name is
556 |       // unknown and can not be inlined.
557 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.NEW) {
558 |         Node target = parent.getFirstChild();
559 |         if (target.getType() == Token.NAME && target.getString().equals(
560 |             ObjectPropertyStringPreprocess.EXTERN_OBJECT_PROPERTY_STRING)) {
561 |           // This method is going to be replaced so don't inline it anywhere.
562 |           fs.setInline(false);
563 |         }
564 |       }
565 | 
566 |       // If the name is being assigned to it can not be inlined.
567 |       if (parent.getType() == Token.ASSIGN && parent.getFirstChild() == n) {
568 |         // e.g. bar = something; <== we can't inline "bar"
569 |         // so mark the function as uninlinable.
570 |         // TODO(johnlenz): Should we just remove it from fns here?
571 |         fs.setInline(false);
572 |       } else {
573 |         // e.g. var fn = bar; <== we can't inline "bar"
574 |         // As this reference can't be inlined mark the function as
575 |         // unremovable.
576 |         fs.setRemove(false);
577 |       }
578 |     }

/**
   * Traverses the current scope and collects declared names.  Does not
   * decent into functions or add CATCH exceptions.
   */
Method code:
187 |   private void findDeclaredNames(Node n, Node parent, Renamer renamer) {
188 |     // Do a shallow traversal, so don't traverse into function declarations,
189 |     // except for the name of the function itself.
190 |     if (parent == null

201 |         findDeclaredNames(c, n, renamer);
202 |       }
203 |     }

===== M0698 =====
Signature: com.google.javascript.rhino.jstype.FunctionType.isEquivalentTo(JSType)
Source: src/com/google/javascript/rhino/jstype/FunctionType.java
Original range: 644-670
Documentation:
/**
   * Two function types are equal if their signatures match. Since they don't
   * have signatures, two interfaces are equal if their names match.
   */
Method code:
644 |   @Override
645 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType otherType) {
646 |     if (!(otherType instanceof FunctionType)) {
647 |       return false;
648 |     }
649 |     FunctionType that = (FunctionType) otherType;
650 |     if (!that.isFunctionType()) {
651 |       return false;
652 |     }
653 |     if (this.isConstructor()) {
654 |       if (that.isConstructor()) {
655 |         return this == that;
656 |       }
657 |       return false;
658 |     }
659 |     if (this.isInterface()) {
660 |       if (that.isInterface()) {
661 |         return this.getReferenceName().equals(that.getReferenceName());
662 |       }
663 |       return false;
664 |     }
665 |     if (that.isInterface()) {
666 |       return false;
667 |     }
668 |     return this.typeOfThis.isEquivalentTo(that.typeOfThis) &&
669 |         this.call.isEquivalentTo(that.call);
670 |   }

149 |   @Override
150 |   public boolean isEquivalentTo(JSType that) {
151 |     if (this == that) {
152 |       return true;
153 |     } else if (this.isNominalType()) {
154 |       ObjectType thatObj = ObjectType.cast(that);

156 |         return getReferenceName().equals(thatObj.getReferenceName());
157 |       }
158 |     }
159 |     return false;
160 |   }

/**
   * Is this node a function declaration? A function declaration is a function
   * that has a name that is added to the current scope (i.e. a function that
   * is not part of a expression; see {@link #isFunctionExpression}).
   */
Method code:

1887 |     return n.getType() == Token.FUNCTION && isStatement(n);
